1	# Paper Scan Processor
2	
3	A desktop application for post-processing PDF files containing batches of scanned documents. The app has two modes: **Merge** and **Split**.
4	
5	## Tech stack
6	
7	- **Desktop framework**: Wails
8	- **Backend**: Go
9	- **Frontend**: TypeScript + React
10	- **UI components**: Mantine
11	- **Virtualization**: TanStack Virtual
12	- **Drag-and-drop**: dnd-kit
13	- **State**: Zustand
14	- **PDF manipulation**: pdfcpu
15	- **PDF rendering** (thumbnails): `mutool` (system install, called as subprocess)
16	
17	## Common elements
18	
19	Both modes include a thumbnail panel and a page detail panel, and support the same per-page editing operations.
20	
21	### Thumbnail panel
22	
23	A vertically scrolling strip of page thumbnails. Width is user-adjustable via a drag handle; thumbnails scale to fill the panel width. Rendering is on-demand and virtualized — only visible thumbnails (plus a small overscan buffer) are rendered at any given time.
24	
25	The Go backend exposes a per-page render method returning a base64-encoded PNG; the frontend requests thumbnails as they scroll into view (`mutool draw` subprocess).
26	
27	#### Keyboard shortcuts
28	
29	| Key                    | Action                                     |
30	| ---------------------- | ------------------------------------------ |
31	| `R`                    | Rotate selected page clockwise 90°         |
32	| `Shift+R`              | Rotate selected page counter-clockwise 90° |
33	| `Delete` / `Backspace` | Toggle skip on the selected page           |
34	| `←` / `→`              | Select previous / next page                |
35	
36	### Page detail panel
37	
38	Shows the currently selected page at reading resolution. Selecting a thumbnail updates it.
39	
40	Supports:
41	
42	- **Pan**: drag to pan.
43	- **Zoom**: scroll wheel or trackpad pinch to zoom in/out.
44	- **Navigate**: `←` / `→` to move to the previous/next page.
45	
46	Implemented with `react-zoom-pan-pinch`.
47	
48	### Page editing
49	
50	Individual pages can be edited in both modes before export or merge:
51	
52	- **Rotation**: pages can be rotated in 90° increments (clockwise or counter-clockwise).
53	- **Skip**: pages can be marked as skipped — excluded from the output but remaining visible in the thumbnail view (greyed out). A page is skipped by clicking a skip icon that appears on hover, or via keyboard shortcut.
54	- **Reorder**: pages can be reordered by dragging thumbnails to a new position.
55	
56	## Mode: Merge
57	
58	For scanners that can only scan one side at a time. The user scans all front pages as one PDF and all back pages as another, then uses Merge mode to interleave them into a single PDF.
59	
60	### Workflow
61	
62	1. The user loads two PDF files, labelled **File A** and **File B**.
63	2. The user selects which file contains the first page (**First page in: File A / File B**).
64	3. A **Reverse File B** checkbox controls whether File B is reversed before interleaving. This should be checked when the paper stack was flipped between scans (the typical case, when scanning one side at a time), causing the second-scanned pages to be in reverse order.
65	4. The app interleaves the pages: A1, B1, A2, B2, etc.
66	5. The user saves the merged result as a new PDF file on disk.
67	
68	The merged PDF can then be opened in Split mode for further processing.
69	
70	### Unequal page counts
71	
72	If File A and File B have different page counts, the app shows a warning before proceeding: "File A has X pages, File B has Y pages. The extra Z page(s) will be appended at the end." The user can cancel or continue. The extra pages from the longer file are appended in order after the interleaved section.
73	
74	### Layout
75	
76	Merge mode uses a two-column layout:
77	
78	- **Left panel** — two side-by-side thumbnail strips, one per input file.
79	- **Right panel** (fills remaining space) — the page detail view, showing whichever page was most recently selected in either thumbnail strip.
80	
81	The thumbnail strip for the file containing the second output page is offset downward by half a thumbnail height. This makes the interleaving order visually apparent: the two files' pages appear to slot between each other.
82	
83	```
84	  A               B
85	  ┌──────────┐
86	  │   A1     │
87	  └──────────┘  ┌──────────┐
88	                │   B1     │
89	  ┌──────────┐  └──────────┘
90	  │   A2     │
91	  └──────────┘  ┌──────────┐
92	                │   B2     │
93	  ┌──────────┐  └──────────┘
94	  │   A3     │
95	  └──────────┘
96	```
97	
98	The offset makes the interleaving order visually apparent without needing labels.
99	
100	### Error handling
101	
102	TBD.
103	
104	## Mode: Split
105	
106	### Workflow
107	
108	1. The user loads an input PDF via a file picker dialog or by dragging and dropping a file onto the app window.
109	2. The app displays all pages as thumbnails in the left panel. Clicking a thumbnail selects it and updates the detail panel on the right.
110	3. The user defines split points by clicking in the gaps between page thumbnails. A visual divider appears at each split point; clicking again removes it. Dividers can also be repositioned by dragging them to a different gap. Each divider marks where a new output file begins.
111	4. The user sets a global filename template using `{date}` (today's date as `YYYY-MM-DD`) and `{name}` (a per-file label). Example: `{date} {name}` → `2026-06-12 invoice.pdf`. The app prefills the filename for each output file using this template. The user can then edit any individual file's name before exporting.
112	5. The user sets a global output folder (an existing folder on the local filesystem). For each output file, the user can adjust:
113	   - The prefilled filename (editable)
114	   - The destination folder (overridable per file)
115	6. The user clicks Export. Before splitting, the app checks for filename conflicts at each destination. If any conflict is found, the export is aborted and an error message identifies the conflicting files. Once resolved, the app splits the input PDF and writes each output file. Afterwards, the app prompts the user to keep, move, or delete the input file. The success modal includes an **"Open in Drive Upload"** button that switches to Drive Upload mode with the output folder pre-set as the root.
116	
117	### Layout
118	
119	Split mode uses a two-column layout:
120	
121	- **Left panel** (adjustable width, drag handle on right edge) — a single vertically scrolling area that combines the thumbnail strip and output file controls.
122	- **Right panel** (fills remaining space) — the page detail view.
123	
124	#### Left panel structure
125	
126	The left panel is a continuous scroll area. Pages are grouped by output file. Each group is preceded by a compact **output file header** containing the editable filename field and (when applicable) destination folder. Split-point dividers between groups are the visual boundary between one output file and the next.
127	
128	```
129	┌─ invoice.pdf ──────────────────────┐
130	│ folder: /Documents                 │
131	└────────────────────────────────────┘
132	  [page 1 thumbnail]
133	  [page 2 thumbnail]
134	  [page 3 thumbnail]
135	  ──────── [gap / split point] ───────
136	┌─ receipt.pdf ──────────────────────┐
137	│ folder: /Documents                 │
138	└────────────────────────────────────┘
139	  [page 4 thumbnail]
140	  [page 5 thumbnail]
141	```
142	
143	Clicking a gap toggles a split point there and creates a new output file section. The filename and folder fields for each section appear immediately above its pages.
144	
145	### Keyboard shortcuts
146	
147	| Key     | Action                                                  |
148	| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
149	| `Space` | Toggle a split point after the selected page            |
150	| `Tab`   | Move focus to the next filename input in the left panel |
151	
152	### Local subfolder (optional)
153	
154	Each output file can be assigned a subfolder name. The file is then saved to `[output folder] / [subfolder] / filename.pdf` rather than directly into the output folder. The user enters the subfolder name manually in the section header. No automatic disambiguation is applied — the user is responsible for avoiding conflicts.
155	
156	### Persisted settings
157	
158	The following settings are saved across sessions:
159	
160	- Last-used output folder
161	- Filename template
162	
163	### Error handling
164	
165	TBD.
166	
167	## Mode: Drive Upload
168	
169	For filing batches of local PDF files to Google Drive. Intended as the step after Split mode: once PDFs are exported to a local folder, the user switches to Drive Upload to route each file (or subfolder of files) to the correct place in Drive.
170	
171	### Workflow
172	
173	1. The user enters Drive Upload mode. If arriving via the Split mode export success modal, the root local folder is pre-set to the Split output folder; otherwise the user picks a root folder.
174	2. The app scans the root folder recursively and displays all files grouped by subfolder. Files in the root folder itself appear as a top-level group.
175	3. The user assigns a Google Drive destination folder to each subfolder group. The assignment propagates to all files within the group. Individual files can override the group's assignment.
176	4. The user can select any file to preview it — the thumbnail strip and detail panel update to show that file's pages.
177	5. The user clicks Upload. Before uploading, the app checks each Drive destination for filename conflicts. If any are found, conflicting files are flagged and the upload is aborted until resolved.
178	6. Uploads proceed with per-file progress. If a file fails, it shows an inline error and a Retry button; other uploads continue unaffected.
179	7. After all uploads complete, each subfolder group shows an "Open in Drive" link to its destination folder.
180	8. The user is prompted to delete or move to a local archive folder the source files that uploaded successfully.
181	
182	### Layout
183	
184	Drive Upload mode uses a three-column layout:
185	
186	- **Left panel** (fixed width) — the file tree: root folder → subfolders → files. Each subfolder header shows an editable name and its Drive destination folder. Each file shows an editable filename, inherits the parent subfolder's Drive destination (with an option to override), and — space permitting — displays file size and page count as secondary metadata. Edited names are the names used on Drive; the local files are not renamed on disk.
187	- **Middle panel** (adjustable width, drag handle on right edge) — thumbnail strip of the currently selected PDF.
188	- **Right panel** (fills remaining space) — the page detail view.
189	
190	```
191	┌──────────────────────────────┐  ┌────────────┐  ┌───────────────────┐
192	│ Root: /output/batch  [Change] │  │            │  │                   │
193	│                               │  │  [page 1]  │  │                   │
194	│ 📁 invoices/                  │  │  [page 2]  │  │   Detail view     │
195	│    Drive: Finance/Invoices ▼  │  │  [page 3]  │  │   (selected page) │
196	│    📄 2026-07-01 invoice.pdf  │  │            │  │                   │
197	│       3 pages · 1.2 MB        │  │            │  │                   │
198	│    📄 2026-07-02 invoice.pdf  │  │            │  │                   │
199	│       2 pages · 0.8 MB        │  │            │  │                   │
200	│                               │  │            │  │                   │
201	│ 📁 receipts/                  │  │            │  │                   │
202	│    Drive: [not assigned] ▼    │  │            │  │                   │
203	│    📄 2026-07-01 receipt.pdf  │  │            │  │                   │
204	│       1 page · 0.4 MB         │  │            │  │                   │
205	│                               │  │            │  │                   │
206	│ 📄 misc.pdf                   │  │            │  │                   │
207	│    Drive: [not assigned] ▼    │  │            │  │                   │
208	│    4 pages · 2.1 MB           │  │            │  │                   │
209	│                               │  │            │  │                   │
210	│ [Upload All]                  │  │            │  │                   │
211	└──────────────────────────────┘  └────────────┘  └───────────────────┘
212	```
213	
214	Clicking a subfolder header selects all files in that group. Clicking a file selects it and loads its pages into the thumbnail strip and detail panel.
215	
216	### Drive folder assignment
217	
218	- Clicking a Drive folder field (on a subfolder header or an individual file) opens a **folder browser modal** displaying the user's Drive folder tree, fetched lazily on first open. A **recently used folders** list appears at the top for quick access.
219	- Selecting multiple items (files and/or subfolders) and assigning a Drive folder applies it to all selected items at once.
220	- A subfolder's Drive folder assignment propagates to all files within it. A file-level assignment overrides the parent subfolder's.
221	
222	### Remembered folder mappings
223	
224	The app remembers Drive folder assignments keyed on local subfolder name. When a subfolder with a previously seen name is loaded, the app auto-fills its Drive destination. The user can change it before uploading. Mappings persist across sessions.
225	
226	### Post-upload cleanup
227	
228	After a successful upload batch, the user is prompted to either delete the source files or move them to a local archive folder. The archive folder destination can be configured and is remembered across sessions.
229	
230	### Persisted settings
231	
232	- Drive folder mappings (local subfolder name → Drive folder)
233	- Recently used Drive folders
234	- Last-used root local folder
235	- Local archive folder path
236	
237	### Authentication
238	
239	Google OAuth via a browser window, triggered the first time Drive Upload mode is used. Credentials are stored locally and reused in future sessions.
240	
241	### Error handling
242	
243	If a file upload fails, the error is shown inline next to that file in the left panel. Other uploads in the batch continue unaffected. The user can retry failed uploads individually without restarting the batch.
244	
245	## Implementation checklist
246	
247	### Primitives
248	
249	- [x] **Go: PDF merge/split** — interleave and split PDFs with pdfcpu; hardcoded paths; `_test.go` harness, no UI
250	- [x] **Frontend: Thumbnail panel** — virtualized vertical scroll, on-demand per-page render via Go/mutool, resizable width with drag handle
251	- [x] **Frontend: Detail panel** — pan and zoom with `react-zoom-pan-pinch`, driven by selected page
252	
253	### Merge mode
254	
255	- [x] **Step 1: End-to-end merge pipeline** — Go `OpenFileDialog` / `SaveFileDialog` RPCs; `MergePDFs` Wails RPC; minimal UI with two file-picker buttons and a Merge & Save button; no thumbnails
256	- [x] **Step 2: Thumbnail strips** — two-column layout using existing `ThumbnailPanel` primitive, one per file, side by side; Merge & Save moves to toolbar
257	- [x] **Step 3: A/B selector + visual offset** — "First page in" toggle (File A / File B); second strip offset down by half a thumbnail height
258	- [x] **Step 4: Reverse checkbox + page-count warning** — "Reverse File B" checkbox wired into merge call; detect unequal counts and show info in toolbar
259	- [x] **Step 5: Detail panel** — existing `DetailPanel` primitive on the right; selection in either strip updates it; `←` / `→` keyboard navigation
260	- [x] **Step 6: Page editing** — rotate and skip within the merge view, applied before the merge call
261	
262	### Split mode
263	
264	- [x] **Step 1: Split points UI** — resizable left panel + detail panel; clickable gaps between thumbnails toggle a visual divider; no drag-and-drop yet
265	- [x] **Step 2: Export pipeline** — Go split/export backend; Export button wired up; no filename control yet
266	- [x] **Step 3: Filename inputs** — per-output-file editable filename field in each section header
267	- [x] **Step 4: Filename prefill** — global filename template with `{date}` / `{name}` tokens; prefills each section header on creation
268	- [x] **Step 5: Output folder** — global output folder picker; per-file folder override in section header
269	- [ ] ~~Step 6: Divider drag-and-drop — reposition or delete dividers by dragging~~
270	- [x] **Step 7: Rotate pages**
271	- [x] **Step 8: Skip pages**
272	- [x] **Step 9a: Reorder pages via up/down buttons**
273	- [x] **Step 9b: Reorder pages via drag and drop**
274	- [x] **Step 10: Export polish** — filename conflict check before export; keep/move/delete prompt for input file after export; "Open in Drive Upload" button in success modal
275	- [ ] **Step 11: Keyboard shortcuts** — `Space` to toggle split point, `Tab` to move between filename inputs
276	- [ ] **Step 12: Persisted settings** — last-used output folder, filename template
277	
278	### Drive Upload mode
279	
280	- [ ] **Step 1a: OAuth authentication** — Go backend only, no UI; OAuth via system default browser with localhost callback; credentials stored locally and reused across sessions
281	- [ ] **Step 1b: Folder listing** — list contents of a hardcoded root folder and a hardcoded subfolder via Drive API
282	- [ ] **Step 1c: File upload** — upload a hardcoded local file to a hardcoded Drive folder
283	- [ ] **Step 2: Filesystem scan + file tree UI** — root folder picker; recursive scan and display of files grouped by subfolder; file size and page count as secondary metadata
284	- [ ] **Step 3: Drive folder assignment UI + inline renaming** — folder browser modal with lazy-loaded Drive tree and recently used list; assignment at subfolder and file level; batch assignment for multi-select; inline editable name for each subfolder and file (controls the Drive upload name, not the local filename)
285	- [ ] **Step 4: PDF preview** — selecting a file loads it into the middle thumbnail strip and right detail panel (reuses existing primitives)
286	- [ ] **Step 5: Upload queue** — per-file upload with progress; inline error + Retry on failure; "Open in Drive" link per group after completion
287	- [ ] **Step 6: Remembered folder mappings** — auto-fill Drive destination from saved subfolder-name→Drive-folder mapping; persisted across sessions
288	- [ ] **Step 7: Post-upload cleanup** — prompt to delete or archive source files; archive moves files to a user-specified local archive folder
289	- [ ] **Step 8: Conflict detection** — check Drive for filename conflicts before uploading; flag conflicting files
290	- [ ] **Step 9: Keychain storage** — store the Drive refresh token in the macOS Keychain instead of a plain JSON file, so it is encrypted at rest and not readable by other user-level processes
291	
292	### Code cleanup
293	
294	- [x] **Move `pageCache.ts` to `src/lib/`** — it's a module-level singleton, not a hook; only `usePageCacheRender` is a hook
295	- [x] **Move `usePDFFile.ts` into `MergeMode/`** — only used by MergeMode; SplitMode re-implements `rotate`/`toggleSkip` independently, so the hook is not truly shared
296	- [x] **Fix duplicate global keydown handlers** — both `SplitMode/ThumbnailPanel` and `DetailPanel` listen to `ArrowLeft`/`ArrowRight`/`Delete` on `window`; after page reordering, the panel navigates by visual order while DetailPanel navigates by numeric order — the last-registered handler wins, producing wrong navigation; remove the overlapping keys from `DetailPanel` and handle them only in the mode-level panel
297	- [x] **Remove unused packages** — `@mantine/dropzone` (CSS imported in `main.tsx` but component never used) and `zustand` (in `package.json` but never imported) can both be removed
298	- [ ] ~~**Wrap `toggle` in `useCallback` in `useOutputFiles.ts:25`** — the only exported function not memoized; causes `handleToggleSplitPoint` (which depends on it) to recreate every render despite its own `useCallback`~~
299	- [ ] ~~**Memoize `getSplitPoints` in `useOutputFiles.ts:79`** — creates a new `Set` on every call; make it a `useMemo` like `duplicateFirstPages`~~
300	- [x] **Name the magic pixel offsets in `MergeMode/index.tsx`** — `totalWidth - 22` and `colWidth + 26` are two different values for what comments describe as "scrollbar + gap"; name them as constants and reconcile
301	- [x] **Add `out.Sync()` in `pdf.go:copyFile`** — without a sync before close, a crash between write completion and OS flush can silently corrupt the output PDF
302	- [x] **Wrap `ThumbColumn` return in a Fragment** — currently returns a raw `JSX.Element[]` array; wrapping in `<>...</>` is more conventional and clarifies intent
303	
304	## Various
305	- Fixes for keyboard naviation:
306	  - [ ] in MergeMode navigation is implemented in index.ts, in SplitMode it's implemented in ThumbnailPanel.tsx. That's inconsistent
307	  - [ ] Change going to previous / next page to up / down arrows
308	  - [ ] Make MergeMode previous / next action to follow the order of the target PDF; meaning to go back and forth between file A and B
309	
310	
311	## Future / out of scope for v1
312	
313	- **Insert pages from another PDF**: allow the user to pull pages from a second PDF into the current document before splitting. Planned for v2.
314	- **Scanning integration**: trigger a scan from within the app using OS or device APIs. Not currently planned but under consideration.
315	- **Page reordering**: drag thumbnails to reorder pages before export or merge. No clear use case identified for the current workflow; omitted from v1.
316

1	package main

16	// App struct
17	type App struct {
18		ctx context.Context
19	}
20	
21	// NewApp creates a new App application struct
22	func NewApp() *App {
23		return &App{}
24	}
25	
26	// startup is called when the app starts. The context is saved
27	// so we can call the runtime methods
28	func (a *App) startup(ctx context.Context) {
29		a.ctx = ctx
30	}
31	
32	// OpenPDF shows a file-open dialog filtered to PDFs and returns the selected path.
33	// Returns an empty string if the user cancels.
34	func (a *App) OpenPDF() (string, error) {
35		path, err := runtime.OpenFileDialog(a.ctx, runtime.OpenDialogOptions{
36			Title: "Open PDF",
37			Filters: []runtime.FileFilter{
38				{DisplayName: "PDF Files (*.pdf)", Pattern: "*.pdf"},
39			},
40		})
41		return path, err
42	}
43	
44	// PageCount returns the number of pages in the PDF at path.
45	func (a *App) PageCount(path string) (int, error) {
46		return pdfPageCount(path)
47	}
48	
49	// RenderPage renders a single page of the PDF at path as a PNG and returns it
50	// base64-encoded. widthPx controls the output width; height is scaled proportionally.
51	func (a *App) RenderPage(path string, pageNum int, widthPx int) (string, error) {
52		tmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp("", "psp-render-*")
53		if err != nil {
54			return "", err
55		}
56		defer os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)
57	
58		outPrefix := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "page")
59		page := strconv.Itoa(pageNum)
60	
61		out, err := exec.Command(
62			"pdftoppm",
63			"-f", page,
64			"-l", page,
65			"-singlefile",
66			"-png",
67			"-scale-to-x", strconv.Itoa(widthPx),
68			"-scale-to-y", "-1",
69			path,
70			outPrefix,
71		).CombinedOutput()
72		if err != nil {
73			return "", fmt.Errorf("pdftoppm: %w\n%s", err, out)
74		}
75	
76		data, err := os.ReadFile(outPrefix + ".png")
77		if err != nil {
78			return "", err
79		}
80		return base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(data), nil
81	}
82	
83	// SavePDF shows a save-file dialog filtered to PDFs and returns the chosen path.
84	// Returns an empty string if the user cancels.
85	func (a *App) SavePDF() (string, error) {
86		path, err := runtime.SaveFileDialog(a.ctx, runtime.SaveDialogOptions{
87			Title:           "Save Merged PDF",
88			DefaultFilename: "merged.pdf",
89			Filters: []runtime.FileFilter{
90				{DisplayName: "PDF Files (*.pdf)", Pattern: "*.pdf"},
91			},
92		})
93		if err != nil || path == "" {
94			return path, err
95		}
96		if !strings.HasSuffix(strings.ToLower(path), ".pdf") {
97			path += ".pdf"
98		}
99		return path, nil
100	}
101	
102	// OpenFile opens the file at path with the system default application.
103	func (a *App) OpenFile(path string) error {
104		return exec.Command("open", path).Run()
105	}
106	
107	// DeleteFile removes the file at path.
108	func (a *App) DeleteFile(path string) error {
109		return os.Remove(path)
110	}
111	
112	// MergePDFs interleaves pages from pathA and pathB and writes the result to outPath.
113	func (a *App) MergePDFs(pathA, pathB, outPath string, firstPageInA, reverseB bool, skipA, skipB []int, rotationsA, rotationsB map[int]int) error {
114		return mergePDFs(pathA, pathB, outPath, firstPageInA, reverseB, skipA, skipB, rotationsA, rotationsB)
115	}
116	
117	// PickFolder shows a folder-select dialog and returns the chosen path.
118	// Returns an empty string if the user cancels.
119	func (a *App) PickFolder() (string, error) {
120		return runtime.OpenDirectoryDialog(a.ctx, runtime.OpenDialogOptions{
121			Title: "Choose Output Folder",
122		})
123	}
124	
125	// OutputFileSpec describes one output file for ExportSplit.
126	type OutputFileSpec struct {
127		Pages  []int  `json:"pages"`  // ordered 1-indexed original page numbers (skip already filtered by caller)
128		Name   string `json:"name"`   // filename without extension; falls back to "output-N" if empty
129		OutDir string `json:"outDir"` // destination directory
130	}
131	
132	// outputFileDest returns the destination path for the i-th OutputFileSpec.
133	func outputFileDest(files []OutputFileSpec, i int) string {
134		name := strings.TrimSpace(files[i].Name)
135		if name == "" {
136			name = fmt.Sprintf("output-%d", i+1)
137		}
138		if !strings.HasSuffix(strings.ToLower(name), ".pdf") {
139			name += ".pdf"
140		}
141		return filepath.Join(files[i].OutDir, name)
142	}
143	
144	// CheckConflicts returns the destination paths that already exist on disk.
145	func (a *App) CheckConflicts(files []OutputFileSpec) ([]string, error) {
146		conflicts := []string{}
147		for i := range files {
148			dest := outputFileDest(files, i)
149			if _, err := os.Stat(dest); err == nil {
150				conflicts = append(conflicts, dest)
151			}
152		}
153		return conflicts, nil
154	}
155	
156	// ExportSplit splits the PDF at inPath according to files and writes each
157	// segment to its OutDir using its Name.
158	// Each file's Pages lists the original (1-indexed) page numbers in display
159	// order; skip filtering and reordering have already been applied by the caller.
160	// rotations maps 1-indexed page numbers to clockwise degrees (90, 180, 270).
161	func (a *App) ExportSplit(inPath string, files []OutputFileSpec, rotations map[int]int) error {
162		tmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp("", "psp-split-*")
163		if err != nil {
164			return err
165		}
166		defer os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)
167	
168		paths, err := splitPDF(inPath, files, rotations, tmpDir)
169		if err != nil {
170			return err
171		}
172	
173		for i, src := range paths {
174			if err := copyFile(src, outputFileDest(files, i)); err != nil {
175				return err
176			}
177		}
178		return nil
179	}
180

1	package main
2	
3	import (
4		"fmt"
5		"io"
6		"os"
7		"path/filepath"
8		"slices"
9		"sort"
10		"strings"
11	
12		"github.com/pdfcpu/pdfcpu/pkg/api"
13	)
14	
15	// mergePDFs interleaves pages from pathA and pathB into outPath.
16	// If firstPageInA is true, file A contributes the odd-numbered output pages (1, 3, 5, …);
17	// otherwise file B does. If reverseB is true, file B's pages are reversed before interleaving,
18	// which is the typical case when the paper stack was flipped between scans.
19	// If the page counts differ, the extra pages from the longer file are
20	// appended in order after the interleaved section.
21	func mergePDFs(pathA, pathB, outPath string, firstPageInA, reverseB bool, skipA, skipB []int, rotationsA, rotationsB map[int]int) error {
22		tmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp("", "psp-merge-*")
23		if err != nil {
24			return err
25		}
26		defer os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)
27	
28		dirA := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "a")
29		dirB := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "b")
30		for _, d := range []string{dirA, dirB} {
31			if err := os.MkdirAll(d, 0o755); err != nil {
32				return err
33			}
34		}
35	
36		if err := api.SplitFile(pathA, dirA, 1, nil); err != nil {
37			return fmt.Errorf("splitting file A: %w", err)
38		}
39		if err := api.SplitFile(pathB, dirB, 1, nil); err != nil {
40			return fmt.Errorf("splitting file B: %w", err)
41		}
42	
43		pagesA, err := sortedPDFsInDir(dirA)
44		if err != nil {
45			return fmt.Errorf("listing file A pages: %w", err)
46		}
47		pagesB, err := sortedPDFsInDir(dirB)
48		if err != nil {
49			return fmt.Errorf("listing file B pages: %w", err)
50		}
51	
52		pagesA = filterSkipped(pagesA, skipA)
53		pagesB = filterSkipped(pagesB, skipB)
54		if reverseB {
55			slices.Reverse(pagesB)
56		}
57	
58		if err := applyRotations(pagesA, rotationsA); err != nil {
59			return fmt.Errorf("rotating file A pages: %w", err)
60		}
61		if err := applyRotations(pagesB, rotationsB); err != nil {
62			return fmt.Errorf("rotating file B pages: %w", err)
63		}
64	
65		if firstPageInA {
66			return api.MergeCreateFile(interleave(pagesA, pagesB), outPath, false, nil)
67		}
68		return api.MergeCreateFile(interleave(pagesB, pagesA), outPath, false, nil)
69	}
70	
71	func filterSkipped(pages []string, skip []int) []string {
72		if len(skip) == 0 {
73			return pages
74		}
75		skipSet := make(map[int]bool, len(skip))
76		for _, p := range skip {
77			skipSet[p] = true
78		}
79		out := make([]string, 0, len(pages))
80		for i, p := range pages {
81			if !skipSet[i+1] {
82				out = append(out, p)
83			}
84		}
85		return out
86	}
87	
88	// applyRotations rotates individual single-page PDF files in-place according to
89	// the rotations map (1-indexed page number → clockwise degrees: 90, 180, 270).
90	// The page number is derived from the pdfcpu split filename.
91	func applyRotations(pages []string, rotations map[int]int) error {
92		for _, p := range pages {
93			origPage := pdfFromPage(filepath.Base(p))
94			rot, ok := rotations[origPage]
95			if !ok || rot == 0 {
96				continue
97			}
98			tmp := p + ".rot.pdf"
99			if err := api.RotateFile(p, tmp, rot, nil, nil); err != nil {
100				return fmt.Errorf("page %d: %w", origPage, err)
101			}
102			if err := os.Rename(tmp, p); err != nil {
103				return err
104			}
105		}
106		return nil
107	}
108	
109	// splitPDF assembles each output file by extracting pages from inPath in the
110	// order specified by each spec's Pages slice, applying rotations, and writing
111	// the results to outDir. Pages are 1-indexed original page numbers; skip
112	// filtering and reordering have already been applied by the caller.
113	func splitPDF(inPath string, files []OutputFileSpec, rotations map[int]int, outDir string) ([]string, error) {
114		tmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp("", "psp-split-pages-*")
115		if err != nil {
116			return nil, err
117		}
118		defer os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)
119	
120		if err := api.SplitFile(inPath, tmpDir, 1, nil); err != nil {
121			return nil, fmt.Errorf("splitting to single pages: %w", err)
122		}
123		allPages, err := sortedPDFsInDir(tmpDir)
124		if err != nil {
125			return nil, fmt.Errorf("listing single pages: %w", err)
126		}
127	
128		if err := applyRotations(allPages, rotations); err != nil {
129			return nil, fmt.Errorf("rotating pages: %w", err)
130		}
131	
132		var outPaths []string
133		for i, spec := range files {
134			if len(spec.Pages) == 0 {
135				return nil, fmt.Errorf("output file %d has no pages", i+1)
136			}
137			segPages := make([]string, 0, len(spec.Pages))
138			for _, pageNum := range spec.Pages {
139				if pageNum < 1 || pageNum > len(allPages) {
140					return nil, fmt.Errorf("output file %d: page %d out of range", i+1, pageNum)
141				}
142				segPages = append(segPages, allPages[pageNum-1])
143			}
144			outPath := filepath.Join(outDir, fmt.Sprintf("segment-%d.pdf", i+1))
145			if err := api.MergeCreateFile(segPages, outPath, false, nil); err != nil {
146				return nil, fmt.Errorf("building segment %d: %w", i+1, err)
147			}
148			outPaths = append(outPaths, outPath)
149		}
150		return outPaths, nil
151	}
152	
153	// pdfPageCount returns the number of pages in the PDF at path.
154	func pdfPageCount(path string) (int, error) {
155		return api.PageCountFile(path)
156	}
157	
158	// sortedPDFsInDir lists PDF files in dir sorted numerically by the first page
159	// number encoded in pdfcpu's output filenames (e.g. "doc_3-5.pdf" → 3).
160	func sortedPDFsInDir(dir string) ([]string, error) {
161		entries, err := os.ReadDir(dir)
162		if err != nil {
163			return nil, err
164		}
165		type item struct {
166			path     string
167			fromPage int
168		}
169		var files []item
170		for _, e := range entries {
171			if e.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(strings.ToLower(e.Name()), ".pdf") {
172				continue
173			}
174			files = append(files, item{
175				path:     filepath.Join(dir, e.Name()),
176				fromPage: pdfFromPage(e.Name()),
177			})
178		}
179		sort.Slice(files, func(i, j int) bool { return files[i].fromPage < files[j].fromPage })
180		result := make([]string, len(files))
181		for i, f := range files {
182			result[i] = f.path
183		}
184		return result, nil
185	}
186	
187	// interleave merges two slices by alternating elements: a[0], b[0], a[1], b[1], ...
188	// If the slices have different lengths, the remaining elements of the longer
189	// slice are appended in order after the interleaved section.
190	func interleave[T any](a, b []T) []T {
191		n := min(len(a), len(b))
192		out := make([]T, 0, len(a)+len(b))
193		for i := range n {
194			out = append(out, a[i], b[i])
195		}
196		out = append(out, a[n:]...)
197		out = append(out, b[n:]...)
198		return out
199	}
200	
201	func copyFile(src, dst string) error {
202		in, err := os.Open(src)
203		if err != nil {
204			return err
205		}
206		defer in.Close()
207		out, err := os.Create(dst)
208		if err != nil {
209			return err
210		}
211		defer out.Close()
212		if _, err = io.Copy(out, in); err != nil {
213			return err
214		}
215		return out.Sync()
216	}
217	
218	// pdfFromPage extracts the first page number from a pdfcpu split filename
219	// such as "doc_1.pdf" or "doc_3-5.pdf".
220	func pdfFromPage(name string) int {
221		name = strings.TrimSuffix(name, ".pdf")
222		parts := strings.Split(name, "_")
223		if len(parts) == 0 {
224			return 0
225		}
226		rangeStr := parts[len(parts)-1]
227		n := 0
228		fmt.Sscanf(strings.SplitN(rangeStr, "-", 2)[0], "%d", &n)
229		return n
230	}
231	
```

1	package main
2	
3	import (
4		"context"
5		"encoding/json"
6		"fmt"
7		"net/http"
8		"os"
9		"os/exec"
10		"path/filepath"
11	
12		"golang.org/x/oauth2"
13		"golang.org/x/oauth2/google"
14		"google.golang.org/api/drive/v3"
15	)
16	
17	const driveOAuthPort = "8765"
18	const driveOAuthCallbackPath = "/oauth/callback"
19	
20	// driveConfigDir returns (and creates) ~/Library/Application Support/paper-scan-processor.
21	func driveConfigDir() (string, error) {
22		base, err := os.UserConfigDir()
23		if err != nil {
24			return "", err
25		}
26		dir := filepath.Join(base, "paper-scan-processor")
27		return dir, os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o700)
28	}
29	
30	func driveOAuthConfig() (*oauth2.Config, error) {
31		dir, err := driveConfigDir()
32		if err != nil {
33			return nil, err
34		}
35		credPath := filepath.Join(dir, "drive_credentials.json")
36		data, err := os.ReadFile(credPath)
37		if err != nil {
38			return nil, fmt.Errorf("read %s: %w", credPath, err)
39		}
40		cfg, err := google.ConfigFromJSON(data, drive.DriveScope)
41		if err != nil {
42			return nil, fmt.Errorf("parse credentials: %w", err)
43		}
44		// Override to match our local callback server.
45		// In Google Cloud Console, register http://localhost as an authorized redirect URI.
46		cfg.RedirectURL = "http://localhost:" + driveOAuthPort + driveOAuthCallbackPath
47		return cfg, nil
48	}
49	
50	// driveClient returns an authenticated HTTP client for the Google Drive API.
51	// On first call it opens the system default browser to perform the OAuth flow
52	// and saves the resulting token for reuse in future sessions.
53	func driveClient(ctx context.Context) (*http.Client, error) {
54		cfg, err := driveOAuthConfig()
55		if err != nil {
56			return nil, err
57		}
58	
59		dir, err := driveConfigDir()
60		if err != nil {
61			return nil, err
62		}
63		tokenPath := filepath.Join(dir, "drive_token.json")
64	
65		if token, err := driveLoadToken(tokenPath); err == nil {
66			return cfg.Client(ctx, token), nil
67		}
68	
69		token, err := driveRunOAuthFlow(ctx, cfg)
70		if err != nil {
71			return nil, err
72		}
73		if err := driveSaveToken(tokenPath, token); err != nil {
74			return nil, fmt.Errorf("save token: %w", err)
75		}
76		return cfg.Client(ctx, token), nil
77	}
78	
79	func driveRunOAuthFlow(ctx context.Context, cfg *oauth2.Config) (*oauth2.Token, error) {
80		codeCh := make(chan string, 1)
81		errCh := make(chan error, 1)
82	
83		mux := http.NewServeMux()
84		srv := &http.Server{Addr: ":" + driveOAuthPort, Handler: mux}
85	
86		mux.HandleFunc(driveOAuthCallbackPath, func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
87			code := r.URL.Query().Get("code")
88			if code == "" {
89				http.Error(w, "missing code", http.StatusBadRequest)
90				errCh <- fmt.Errorf("OAuth callback: missing code parameter")
91				return
92			}
93			fmt.Fprint(w, "<html><body><h1>Authenticated</h1><p>You can close this tab.</p></body></html>")
94			codeCh <- code
95		})
96	
97		go func() {
98			if err := srv.ListenAndServe(); err != nil && err != http.ErrServerClosed {
99				errCh <- err
100			}
101		}()
102		defer srv.Shutdown(context.Background()) //nolint:errcheck
103	
104		authURL := cfg.AuthCodeURL("", oauth2.AccessTypeOffline)
105		if err := exec.Command("open", authURL).Run(); err != nil {
106			return nil, fmt.Errorf("open browser: %w", err)
107		}
108	
109		select {
110		case code := <-codeCh:
111			return cfg.Exchange(ctx, code)
112		case err := <-errCh:
113			return nil, err
114		case <-ctx.Done():
115			return nil, ctx.Err()
116		}
117	}
118	
119	func driveLoadToken(path string) (*oauth2.Token, error) {
120		f, err := os.Open(path)
121		if err != nil {
122			return nil, err
123		}
124		defer f.Close()
125		var t oauth2.Token
126		return &t, json.NewDecoder(f).Decode(&t)
127	}
128	
129	func driveSaveToken(path string, token *oauth2.Token) error {
130		f, err := os.Create(path)
131		if err != nil {
132			return err
133		}
134		defer f.Close()
135		return json.NewEncoder(f).Encode(token)
136	}
137

1	package main
2	
3	import (
4		"context"
5		"fmt"
6	
7		"google.golang.org/api/drive/v3"
8		"google.golang.org/api/option"
9	)
10	
11	// DriveItem represents a file or folder in Google Drive.
12	type DriveItem struct {
13		ID       string
14		Name     string
15		IsFolder bool
16		Size     int64 // bytes; 0 for folders
17	}
18	
19	func driveService(ctx context.Context) (*drive.Service, error) {
20		client, err := driveClient(ctx)
21		if err != nil {
22			return nil, err
23		}
24		return drive.NewService(ctx, option.WithHTTPClient(client))
25	}
26	
27	// DriveFindFolder returns the ID of the first folder named name within parentID.
28	// Use "root" for the Drive root. Folder names with single quotes are not supported.
29	func DriveFindFolder(ctx context.Context, parentID, name string) (string, error) {
30		svc, err := driveService(ctx)
31		if err != nil {
32			return "", err
33		}
34		q := fmt.Sprintf(
35			"name = '%s' and mimeType = 'application/vnd.google-apps.folder' and '%s' in parents and trashed = false",
36			name, parentID,
37		)
38		result, err := svc.Files.List().Q(q).Fields("files(id, name)").Do()
39		if err != nil {
40			return "", fmt.Errorf("find folder %q: %w", name, err)
41		}
42		if len(result.Files) == 0 {
43			return "", fmt.Errorf("folder %q not found in parent %q", name, parentID)
44		}
45		return result.Files[0].Id, nil
46	}
47	
48	// DriveListFolder returns the direct children of the folder with the given ID,
49	// folders first then files, both sorted by name.
50	// Note: results are capped at 100 items (Drive API default page size).
51	func DriveListFolder(ctx context.Context, folderID string) ([]DriveItem, error) {
52		svc, err := driveService(ctx)
53		if err != nil {
54			return nil, err
55		}
56		q := fmt.Sprintf("'%s' in parents and trashed = false", folderID)
57		result, err := svc.Files.List().
58			Q(q).
59			Fields("files(id, name, mimeType, size)").
60			OrderBy("folder,name").
61			Do()
62		if err != nil {
63			return nil, fmt.Errorf("list folder %q: %w", folderID, err)
64		}
65		items := make([]DriveItem, len(result.Files))
66		for i, f := range result.Files {
67			items[i] = DriveItem{
68				ID:       f.Id,
69				Name:     f.Name,
70				IsFolder: f.MimeType == "application/vnd.google-apps.folder",
71				Size:     f.Size,
72			}
73		}
74		return items, nil
75	}
76

1	package main
2	
3	import (
4		"context"
5		"fmt"
6		"os"
7		"path/filepath"
8	
9		"google.golang.org/api/drive/v3"
10	)
11	
12	// DriveUploadFile uploads the local file at localPath to the Drive folder with
13	// the given ID. The filename on Drive matches the base name of localPath.
14	// Returns the ID of the created Drive file.
15	func DriveUploadFile(ctx context.Context, localPath, driveFolderID string) (string, error) {
16		f, err := os.Open(localPath)
17		if err != nil {
18			return "", fmt.Errorf("open %s: %w", localPath, err)
19		}
20		defer f.Close()
21	
22		svc, err := driveService(ctx)
23		if err != nil {
24			return "", err
25		}
26	
27		meta := &drive.File{
28			Name:    filepath.Base(localPath),
29			Parents: []string{driveFolderID},
30		}
31		created, err := svc.Files.Create(meta).Media(f).Fields("id, name, size").Do()
32		if err != nil {
33			return "", fmt.Errorf("upload %s: %w", localPath, err)
34		}
35		return created.Id, nil
36	}
37

1	import { useRef, useEffect } from 'react'
2	import { TransformWrapper, TransformComponent, ReactZoomPanPinchRef } from 'react-zoom-pan-pinch'
3	import { Center, Loader } from '@mantine/core'
4	import * as pageCache from '../lib/pageCache'
5	
6	const DETAIL_WIDTH = 1400
7	
8	interface Props {
9	  pdfPath: string
10	  pageNum: number
11	  pageCount: number
12	  rotation?: number
13	  onToggleSkip?: () => void
14	  onRotate?: () => void
15	}
16	
17	export default function DetailPanel({ pdfPath, pageNum, pageCount, rotation = 0, onToggleSkip, onRotate }: Props) {
18	  pageCache.usePageCacheRender()
19	  const transformRef = useRef<ReactZoomPanPinchRef>(null)
20	
21	  useEffect(() => {
22	    pageCache.load(pdfPath, pageNum, DETAIL_WIDTH)
23	    if (pageNum > 1) pageCache.load(pdfPath, pageNum - 1, DETAIL_WIDTH) // prefetch previous page
24	    if (pageNum < pageCount) pageCache.load(pdfPath, pageNum + 1, DETAIL_WIDTH) // prefetch next page
25	  }, [pdfPath, pageNum, pageCount])
26	
27	  useEffect(() => {
28	    transformRef.current?.resetTransform()
29	  }, [pageNum, rotation])
30	
31	  useEffect(() => {
32	    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
33	      if (e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement || e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) return
34	      if (e.key === 'r') {
35	        e.preventDefault()
36	        onRotate?.()
37	      }
38	    }
39	    window.addEventListener('keydown', handler)
40	    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handler)
41	  }, [onRotate])
42	
43	  const cachedWidth = pageCache.getCachedWidth(pdfPath, pageNum)
44	  const src = cachedWidth !== undefined && cachedWidth >= DETAIL_WIDTH
45	    ? pageCache.getSrc(pdfPath, pageNum)
46	    : undefined
47	
48	  return (
49	    <div
50	      style={{
51	        flex: 1,
52	        height: '100%',
53	        overflow: 'hidden',
54	        position: 'relative',
55	        background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-1)',
56	        padding: 8,
57	      }}
58	    >
59	      {pageCache.isLoading(pdfPath, pageNum) && (
60	        <Center style={{ position: 'absolute', inset: 0, zIndex: 1 }}>
61	          <Loader />
62	        </Center>
63	      )}
64	      {src && (
65	        <TransformWrapper ref={transformRef} centerOnInit minScale={0.1} maxScale={8}>
66	          <TransformComponent
67	            wrapperStyle={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }}
68	            contentStyle={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}
69	          >
70	            <img
71	              src={src}
72	              alt={`Page ${pageNum}`}
73	              draggable={false}
74	              style={{ maxWidth: '100%', maxHeight: '100%', display: 'block', userSelect: 'none', transform: rotation ? `rotate(${rotation}deg)` : undefined }}
75	            />
76	          </TransformComponent>
77	        </TransformWrapper>
78	      )}
79	    </div>
80	  )
81	}
82

1	import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
2	import { RenderPage } from '../../wailsjs/go/main/App'
3	
4	interface Entry { src: string; width: number }
5	
6	// entries stores the best (highest-width) image loaded so far per page.
7	// loading tracks the width of the currently in-flight render per page; a higher-res
8	// request overwrites the value, which lets the stale lower-res .then know it was superseded.
9	// failed tracks the highest width that failed per page; retries at higher widths are still allowed.
10	const entries = new Map<string, Entry>()
11	const loading = new Map<string, number>()
12	const failed = new Map<string, number>()
13	const listeners = new Set<() => void>()
14	
15	const pageKey = (path: string, page: number) => `${path}\0${page}`
16	
17	function notify() {
18	  for (const fn of listeners) fn()
19	}
20	
21	export function subscribe(fn: () => void): () => void {
22	  listeners.add(fn)
23	  return () => listeners.delete(fn)
24	}
25	
26	export function usePageCacheRender() {
27	  const [, setTick] = useState(0)
28	  useEffect(() => subscribe(() => setTick(t => t + 1)), [])
29	}
30	
31	export function getSrc(path: string, page: number): string | undefined {
32	  return entries.get(pageKey(path, page))?.src
33	}
34	
35	export function getCachedWidth(path: string, page: number): number | undefined {
36	  return entries.get(pageKey(path, page))?.width
37	}
38	
39	export function isLoading(path: string, page: number): boolean {
40	  return loading.has(pageKey(path, page))
41	}
42	
43	export function isFailed(path: string, page: number): boolean {
44	  return failed.has(pageKey(path, page))
45	}
46	
47	export function load(path: string, page: number, width: number): void {
48	  if (!path) return
49	  const k = pageKey(path, page)
50	  const existing = entries.get(k)
51	  if (existing && existing.width >= width) return
52	  const currentlyLoading = loading.get(k)
53	  // Don't start a new render if a higher-res one is already in-flight — it will supersede this one.
54	  if (currentlyLoading !== undefined && currentlyLoading >= width) return
55	  const failedAt = failed.get(k)
56	  if (failedAt !== undefined && failedAt >= width) return
57	  // Overwrite any lower-res in-flight entry. The superseded render will still complete but its
58	  // .then/.catch checks loading.get(k) === width before mutating state, so it becomes a no-op.
59	  loading.set(k, width)
60	  RenderPage(path, page, Math.round(width * window.devicePixelRatio))
61	    .then((b64) => {
62	      // A higher-res render may have completed while this one was in-flight — don't downgrade.
63	      const current = entries.get(k)
64	      if (!current || current.width <= width) {
65	        entries.set(k, { src: `data:image/png;base64,${b64}`, width })
66	      }
67	      // Only clear loading if we're still the active render (not superseded by a higher-res one).
68	      if (loading.get(k) === width) loading.delete(k)
69	      notify()
70	    })
71	    .catch(() => {
72	      if (loading.get(k) === width) {
73	        loading.delete(k)
74	        failed.set(k, width)
75	      }
76	      notify()
77	    })
78	}
79	
80	export function evict(path: string): void {
81	  const prefix = path + '\0'
82	  for (const k of entries.keys()) if (k.startsWith(prefix)) entries.delete(k)
83	  for (const k of loading.keys()) if (k.startsWith(prefix)) loading.delete(k)
84	  for (const k of failed.keys()) if (k.startsWith(prefix)) failed.delete(k)
85	  notify()
86	}
87	
```

18	type ListItem =
19	  | { type: 'header'; fileIndex: number; firstPosition: number }
20	  | { type: 'page'; page: number; position: number }
21	
22	function buildItems(pageOrder: number[], splitPoints: Set<number>): ListItem[] {
23	  const result: ListItem[] = []
24	  let fileIndex = 0
25	  for (let pos = 0; pos < pageOrder.length; pos++) {
26	    if (pos === 0 || splitPoints.has(pos - 1)) {
27	      result.push({ type: 'header', fileIndex: fileIndex++, firstPosition: pos })
28	    }
29	    result.push({ type: 'page', page: pageOrder[pos], position: pos })
30	  }
31	  return result
32	}

48	}

50	export default function SplitThumbnailPanel({
51	  pdfPath, pageOrder, selectedPage, onSelectPage,
52	  onToggleSplitPoint,
53	  outputFiles,
54	  outputFolder,
55	  focus,
56	  rotations,

60	  onMovePage,
61	}: Props) {
62	  const [panelWidth, setPanelWidth] = useState(DEFAULT_WIDTH)
63	  const [hoveredGap, setHoveredGap] = useState<number | null>(null)
64	  const [hoveredPage, setHoveredPage] = useState<number | null>(null)
65	  const [activeId, setActiveId] = useState<number | null>(null)
66	  const sensors = useSensors(useSensor(PointerSensor, { activationConstraint: { distance: 8 } }))
67	
68	  const thumbWidth = panelWidth - ITEM_PADDING * 2
69	  const thumbHeight = Math.round(thumbWidth * PAGE_ASPECT)
70	  const pageItemHeight = thumbHeight + LABEL_HEIGHT + ITEM_PADDING + GAP_HEIGHT
71	
72	  const splitPoints = useMemo(() => outputFiles.getSplitPoints(), [outputFiles.all])
73	  const items = useMemo(() => buildItems(pageOrder, splitPoints), [pageOrder, splitPoints])
74	  // Ref so the scroll effect can read the current list without depending on it
75	  // (we don't want to re-scroll every time a split point is toggled).
76	  const itemsRef = useRef(items)
77	  itemsRef.current = items
78	
79	  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
80	
81	  const virtualizer = useVirtualizer({
82	    count: items.length,
83	    getScrollElement: () => scrollRef.current,
84	    estimateSize: (index) => items[index]?.type === 'header' ? HEADER_HEIGHT : pageItemHeight,
85	    overscan: 3,
86	  })
87	
88	  useEffect(() => {
89	    virtualizer.measure()
90	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
91	  }, [items.length, pageItemHeight])
92	
93	  const virtualItems = virtualizer.getVirtualItems()
94	  pageCache.usePageCacheRender()
95	
96	  useEffect(() => {
97	    for (const vItem of virtualItems) {
98	      const item = items[vItem.index]
99	      if (item.type === 'page') pageCache.load(pdfPath, item.page, thumbWidth)
100	    }
101	  })
102	
103	  useEffect(() => {
104	    const index = itemsRef.current.findIndex(item => item.type === 'page' && item.page === selectedPage)
105	    if (index >= 0) virtualizer.scrollToIndex(index, { align: 'auto' })
106	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
107	  }, [selectedPage])
108	
109	  useEffect(() => {
110	    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
111	      if (e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement || e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) return
112	      const selectedPos = pageOrder.indexOf(selectedPage)
113	      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
114	        e.preventDefault()
115	        if (selectedPos > 0) onSelectPage(pageOrder[selectedPos - 1])
116	      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
117	        e.preventDefault()
118	        if (selectedPos < pageOrder.length - 1) onSelectPage(pageOrder[selectedPos + 1])
119	      } else if (e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') {
120	        e.preventDefault()
121	        onToggleSkip(selectedPage)
122	      }
123	    }
124	    window.addEventListener('keydown', handler)
125	    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handler)
126	  }, [selectedPage, pageOrder, onSelectPage, onToggleSkip])
127	
128	  const handleDragEnd = ({ active, over }: DragEndEvent) => {
129	    setActiveId(null)
130	    if (!over || active.id === over.id) return
131	    const fromPos = pageOrder.indexOf(active.id as number)
132	    const toPos = pageOrder.indexOf(over.id as number)
133	    if (fromPos !== -1 && toPos !== -1) onMovePage(fromPos, toPos)
134	  }
135	
136	  const startDrag = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
137	    const startX = e.clientX
138	    const startWidth = panelWidth
139	    const clamp = (w: number) => Math.max(MIN_WIDTH, Math.min(MAX_WIDTH, w))
140	    const onMove = (ev: MouseEvent) => setPanelWidth(clamp(startWidth + ev.clientX - startX))
141	    const onUp = (ev: MouseEvent) => {
142	      setPanelWidth(clamp(startWidth + ev.clientX - startX))
143	      document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove)
144	      document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp)
145	    }
146	    document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove)
147	    document.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp)
148	    e.preventDefault()
149	  }
150	
151	  return (
152	    <div style={{ display: 'flex', height: '100%', flexShrink: 0 }}>
153	      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', width: panelWidth, height: '100%' }}>
154	        <DndContext
155	          sensors={sensors}
156	          collisionDetection={closestCenter}
157	          onDragStart={({ active }) => setActiveId(active.id as number)}
158	          onDragEnd={handleDragEnd}
159	        >
160	          <SortableContext items={pageOrder} strategy={verticalListSortingStrategy}>
161	            <div
162	              ref={scrollRef}
163	              style={{
164	                flex: 1,
165	                minHeight: 0,
166	                overflowY: 'auto',
167	                overflowX: 'hidden',
168	                background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',
169	              }}
170	            >
171	              <div style={{ height: virtualizer.getTotalSize(), position: 'relative' }}>
172	                {virtualItems.map(vItem => {
173	                  const item = items[vItem.index]
174	
175	                  if (item.type === 'header') {
176	                    return (
177	                      <div
178	                        key={vItem.key}
179	                        style={{ position: 'absolute', top: vItem.start, left: 0, width: '100%', height: vItem.size }}
180	                      >
181	                        <OutputFileHeader
182	                          filename={outputFiles.all.get(item.firstPosition)?.name ?? ''}
183	                          onChange={(name) => outputFiles.setName(item.firstPosition, name)}
184	                          firstPosition={item.firstPosition}
185	                          focus={focus}
186	                          folder={outputFiles.all.get(item.firstPosition)?.folderOverride ?? outputFolder}
187	                          onPickFolder={() => outputFiles.pickFolderOverride(item.firstPosition)}
188	                          isDuplicate={outputFiles.duplicateFirstPages.has(item.firstPosition)}
189	                        />
190	                      </div>
191	                    )
192	                  }
193	
194	                  const { page, position } = item
195	                  const isSplit = splitPoints.has(position)
196	                  const isLastPage = position === pageOrder.length - 1
197	
198	                  return (
199	                    <div
200	                      key={vItem.key}
201	                      style={{
202	                        position: 'absolute',
203	                        top: vItem.start,
204	                        left: 0,
205	                        width: '100%',
206	                        height: vItem.size,
207	                        boxSizing: 'border-box',
208	                      }}
209	                      onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredPage(page)}
210	                      onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredPage(null)}
211	                    >
212	                      <SortablePageItem id={page}>
213	                        <PageThumbnail
214	                          src={pageCache.getSrc(pdfPath, page)}
215	                          pdfPath={pdfPath}
216	                          page={page}
217	                          thumbHeight={thumbHeight}
218	                          isSelected={page === selectedPage}
219	                          isSkipped={skipped.has(page)}
220	                          rotation={rotations.get(page) ?? 0}
221	                          isHovered={hoveredPage === page}
222	                          label={String(page)}
223	                          onClick={() => onSelectPage(page)}
224	                          onRotate={() => onRotate(page)}
225	                          onToggleSkip={() => onToggleSkip(page)}
226	                          canMoveUp={position > 0}
227	                          canMoveDown={!isLastPage}
228	                          onMoveUp={() => onMovePage(position, position - 1)}
229	                          onMoveDown={() => onMovePage(position, position + 1)}
230	                        />
231	                      </SortablePageItem>
232	                      {!isLastPage && (
233	                        <GapZone
234	                          isSplit={isSplit}
235	                          isHovered={hoveredGap === position}
236	                          onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onToggleSplitPoint(position) }}
237	                          onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredGap(position)}
238	                          onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredGap(null)}
239	                        />
240	                      )}
241	                    </div>
242	                  )
243	                })}
244	              </div>
245	            </div>
246	          </SortableContext>
247	          <DragOverlay>
248	            {activeId !== null && (
249	              <PageThumbnail
250	                src={pageCache.getSrc(pdfPath, activeId)}
251	                pdfPath={pdfPath}
252	                page={activeId}
253	                thumbHeight={thumbHeight}
254	                isSelected={false}
255	                isSkipped={skipped.has(activeId)}
256	                rotation={rotations.get(activeId) ?? 0}
257	                isHovered={false}
258	                label={String(activeId)}
259	                onClick={() => {}}
260	                onRotate={() => {}}
261	                onToggleSkip={() => {}}
262	              />
263	            )}
264	          </DragOverlay>
265	        </DndContext>
266	      </div>
267	
268	      <div
269	        onMouseDown={startDrag}
270	        style={{
271	          width: DRAG_HANDLE_WIDTH,
272	          height: '100%',
273	          cursor: 'col-resize',
274	          flexShrink: 0,
275	          background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',
276	        }}
277	      />
278	    </div>
279	  )
280	}
281	
282	function SortablePageItem({ id, children }: { id: number; children: React.ReactNode }) {
283	  const { setNodeRef, transform, isDragging, attributes, listeners } = useSortable({ id })
284	  return (
285	    <div
286	      ref={setNodeRef}
287	      style={{ transform: CSS.Transform.toString(transform), opacity: isDragging ? 0 : 1 }}
288	      {...attributes}
289	      {...listeners}
290	    >
291	      {children}
292	    </div>
293	  )
294	}
295	
296	interface GapZoneProps {
297	  isSplit: boolean
298	  isHovered: boolean
299	  onClick: (e: React.MouseEvent) => void
300	  onMouseEnter: () => void
301	  onMouseLeave: () => void
302	}
303	
304	function GapZone({ isSplit, isHovered, onClick, onMouseEnter, onMouseLeave }: GapZoneProps) {
305	  const bg = isSplit
306	    ? 'var(--mantine-color-blue-0)'
307	    : isHovered
308	    ? 'var(--mantine-color-gray-2)'
309	    : undefined
310	
311	  return (
312	    <div
313	      onClick={onClick}
314	      onMouseEnter={onMouseEnter}
315	      onMouseLeave={onMouseLeave}
316	      style={{
317	        height: GAP_HEIGHT,
318	        background: bg,
319	        display: 'flex',
320	        alignItems: 'center',
321	        paddingLeft: ITEM_PADDING,
322	        paddingRight: ITEM_PADDING,
323	        cursor: 'pointer',
324	      }}
325	    >
326	      {isSplit && (
327	        <div style={{ flex: 1, height: 2, background: 'var(--mantine-color-blue-5)', borderRadius: 1 }} />
328	      )}
329	      {!isSplit && isHovered && (
330	        <div style={{ flex: 1, height: 0, borderTop: '1px dashed var(--mantine-color-gray-5)' }} />
331	      )}
332	    </div>
333	  )
334	}
335

2	import { useVirtualizer } from '@tanstack/react-virtual'

8	
9	const MIN_TOTAL_WIDTH = 240
10	const MAX_TOTAL_WIDTH = 960
11	export const DEFAULT_TOTAL_WIDTH = DEFAULT_WIDTH * 2
12	
13	export type FirstPageIn = 'a' | 'b'
14	export type SelectedPage = { file: FirstPageIn, page: number }
15	
16	interface Props {
17	  fileA: PDFFile
18	  fileB: PDFFile
19	  selectedPage: SelectedPage
20	  onSelectPage: (file: FirstPageIn, page: number) => void
21	  firstPageIn: FirstPageIn

23	  onWidthChange: (w: number) => void
24	  colWidth: number
25	  reverseB: boolean
26	}
27	
28	function makePageNumberLabel(isFirst: boolean, countOther: number) {
29	  return (index: number) => {
30	    const n = index + 1
31	    if (n <= countOther) return isFirst ? 2 * n - 1 : 2 * n
32	    return 2 * countOther + (n - countOther)
33	  }
34	}
35	
36	export default function MergeModeThumbnailPanel({
37	  fileA, fileB,
38	  selectedPage, onSelectPage,
39	  firstPageIn, totalWidth, onWidthChange, colWidth, reverseB,
40	}: Props) {
41	  const selectedPageA = selectedPage.file === 'a' ? selectedPage.page : null
42	  const selectedPageB = selectedPage.file === 'b' ? selectedPage.page : null
43	  const thumbWidth = colWidth - ITEM_PADDING * 2
44	  const thumbHeight = Math.round(thumbWidth * PAGE_ASPECT)
45	  const itemHeight = thumbHeight + LABEL_HEIGHT + ITEM_PADDING
46	
47	  const bothLoaded = fileA.path !== null && fileB.path !== null
48	  const halfThumbHeight = Math.round(thumbHeight / 2)
49	  const offsetA = bothLoaded && firstPageIn === 'b' ? halfThumbHeight : 0
50	  const offsetB = bothLoaded && firstPageIn === 'a' ? halfThumbHeight : 0
51	
52	  const totalHeight = Math.max(offsetA + fileA.count * itemHeight, offsetB + fileB.count * itemHeight, 0)
53	
54	  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
55	
56	  pageCache.usePageCacheRender()
57	
58	  const aIsFirst = firstPageIn === 'a'
59	  const pageLabelA = bothLoaded ? makePageNumberLabel(aIsFirst, aIsFirst ? fileB.count : fileA.count) : undefined
60	  const pageLabelB = bothLoaded ? makePageNumberLabel(!aIsFirst, aIsFirst ? fileA.count : fileB.count) : undefined
61	
62	  const startDrag = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
63	    const startX = e.clientX
64	    const startWidth = totalWidth
65	    const clamp = (w: number) => Math.max(MIN_TOTAL_WIDTH, Math.min(MAX_TOTAL_WIDTH, w))
66	
67	    const onMove = (ev: MouseEvent) => onWidthChange(clamp(startWidth + ev.clientX - startX))
68	    const onUp = (ev: MouseEvent) => {
69	      onWidthChange(clamp(startWidth + ev.clientX - startX))
70	      document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove)
71	      document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp)
72	    }
73	    document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove)
74	    document.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp)
75	    e.preventDefault()
76	  }
77	
78	  return (
79	    <Box style={{ display: 'flex', height: '100%', flexShrink: 0 }}>
80	      <Box style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', width: totalWidth, height: '100%' }}>
81	        {/* Single scroll area with two absolute columns */}
82	        <div
83	          ref={scrollRef}
84	          style={{
85	            flex: 1,
86	            minHeight: 0,
87	            overflowY: 'auto',
88	            overflowX: 'hidden',
89	            background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',
90	          }}
91	        >
92	          <div style={{ height: totalHeight, position: 'relative' }}>
93	            <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0, width: colWidth, height: '100%' }}>
94	              <ThumbColumn
95	                scrollRef={scrollRef}
96	                file={fileA}
97	                pdfPath={fileA.path ?? ''}
98	                itemHeight={itemHeight}
99	                paddingStart={offsetA}
100	                thumbWidth={thumbWidth}
101	                thumbHeight={thumbHeight}
102	                selectedPage={selectedPageA}
103	                onSelectPage={(page) => onSelectPage('a', page)}
104	                pageLabel={pageLabelA}
105	              />
106	            </div>
107	            <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: colWidth, top: 0, width: colWidth, height: '100%' }}>
108	              <ThumbColumn
109	                scrollRef={scrollRef}
110	                file={fileB}
111	                pdfPath={fileB.path ?? ''}
112	                itemHeight={itemHeight}
113	                paddingStart={offsetB}
114	                thumbWidth={thumbWidth}
115	                thumbHeight={thumbHeight}
116	                selectedPage={selectedPageB}
117	                onSelectPage={(page) => onSelectPage('b', page)}
118	                pageLabel={pageLabelB}
119	                reverse={reverseB}
120	              />
121	            </div>
122	          </div>
123	        </div>
124	      </Box>
125	
126	      {/* Resize drag handle */}
127	      <div
128	        onMouseDown={startDrag}
129	        style={{
130	          width: DRAG_HANDLE_WIDTH,
131	          height: '100%',
132	          cursor: 'col-resize',
133	          flexShrink: 0,
134	          background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',
135	        }}
136	      />
137	    </Box>
138	  )
139	}

141	interface ThumbColumnProps {
142	  scrollRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>
143	  file: PDFFile
144	  pdfPath: string
145	  itemHeight: number
146	  paddingStart: number
147	  thumbWidth: number
148	  thumbHeight: number
149	  selectedPage: number | null
150	  onSelectPage: (page: number) => void
151	  pageLabel?: (index: number) => number
152	  reverse?: boolean
153	}
154	
155	function ThumbColumn({
156	  scrollRef, file, pdfPath, itemHeight, paddingStart,
157	  thumbWidth, thumbHeight,
158	  selectedPage, onSelectPage, pageLabel,
159	  reverse,
160	}: ThumbColumnProps) {
161	  const { count, skipped, rotations, toggleSkip, rotate } = file
162	  const pageAt = (index: number) => reverse ? count - index : index + 1
163	  const [hoveredPage, setHoveredPage] = useState<number | null>(null)
164	  const virtualizer = useVirtualizer({
165	    count,
166	    getScrollElement: () => scrollRef.current,
167	    estimateSize: () => itemHeight,
168	    overscan: 3,
169	    paddingStart,
170	  })
171	
172	  useEffect(() => {
173	    virtualizer.measure()
174	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
175	  }, [itemHeight])
176	
177	  useEffect(() => {
178	    for (const item of virtualizer.getVirtualItems()) pageCache.load(pdfPath, pageAt(item.index), thumbWidth)
179	  })
180	
181	  useEffect(() => {
182	    if (selectedPage !== null && count > 0) {
183	      const displayIndex = reverse ? count - selectedPage : selectedPage - 1
184	      virtualizer.scrollToIndex(displayIndex, { align: 'auto' })
185	    }
186	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
187	  }, [selectedPage])
188	
189	  return (
190	    <>
191	      {virtualizer.getVirtualItems().map(item => {
192	        const page = pageAt(item.index)
193	        return (
194	          <div
195	            key={item.key}
196	            onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredPage(page)}
197	            onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredPage(null)}
198	            style={{
199	              position: 'absolute',
200	              top: item.start,
201	              left: 0,
202	              width: '100%',
203	              height: item.size,
204	              boxSizing: 'border-box',
205	            }}
206	          >
207	            <PageThumbnail
208	              src={pageCache.getSrc(pdfPath, page)}
209	              pdfPath={pdfPath}
210	              page={page}
211	              thumbHeight={thumbHeight}
212	              isSelected={page === selectedPage}
213	              isSkipped={skipped.has(page)}
214	              rotation={rotations.get(page) ?? 0}
215	              isHovered={hoveredPage === page}
216	              label={String(pageLabel ? pageLabel(item.index) : page)}
217	              onClick={() => onSelectPage(page)}
218	              onRotate={() => rotate(page)}
219	              onToggleSkip={() => toggleSkip(page)}
220	            />
221	          </div>
222	        )
223	      })}
224	    </>
225	  )
226	}
227	    
228	
229

1	import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react'
2	import { Box, Button, Divider, Group, Modal, Stack, Text, TextInput } from '@mantine/core'
3	import { notifications } from '@mantine/notifications'
4	import ThumbnailPanel from './ThumbnailPanel'
5	import DetailPanel from '../DetailPanel'
6	import { OpenFile, OpenPDF, PageCount, PickFolder, ExportSplit, CheckConflicts, DeleteFile } from '../../../wailsjs/go/main/App'
7	import { ellipsisPath } from '../../utils'
8	import { useOutputFiles } from './useOutputFiles'
9	import { usePendingFocus } from './usePendingFocus'
10	
11	const DEFAULT_TEMPLATE = '{date} {name}'
12	
13	function applyTemplate(template: string): { value: string; cursorPos: number } {
14	  const date = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]
15	  const withDate = template.replace('{date}', date)
16	  const nameIdx = withDate.indexOf('{name}')
17	  if (nameIdx === -1) return { value: withDate, cursorPos: withDate.length }
18	  return { value: withDate.replace('{name}', ''), cursorPos: nameIdx }
19	}
20	
21	interface Props {
22	  initialPath?: string | null
23	}
24	
25	export default function SplitMode({ initialPath }: Props) {
26	  const [pdfPath, setPdfPath] = useState<string | null>(null)
27	  const [pageCount, setPageCount] = useState(0)
28	  const [pageOrder, setPageOrder] = useState<number[]>([])
29	  const [selectedPage, setSelectedPage] = useState(1)
30	  const [outputFolder, setOutputFolder] = useState<string | null>(null)
31	  const [successModal, setSuccessModal] = useState<{show: boolean, outputPath: string, inputPath: string}>({show: false, outputPath: '', inputPath: ''})
32	  const outputFiles = useOutputFiles(outputFolder)
33	  const focus = usePendingFocus()
34	  const [template, setTemplate] = useState(DEFAULT_TEMPLATE)
35	  const [exporting, setExporting] = useState(false)
36	  const [rotations, setRotations] = useState<Map<number, number>>(() => new Map())
37	  const [skipped, setSkipped] = useState<Set<number>>(() => new Set())
38	
39	  const rotate = (page: number) => {
40	    setRotations(prev => {
41	      const next = new Map(prev)
42	      const deg = ((next.get(page) ?? 0) + 90) % 360
43	      if (deg === 0) next.delete(page); else next.set(page, deg)
44	      return next
45	    })
46	  }
47	
48	  const toggleSkip = (page: number) => {
49	    setSkipped(prev => {
50	      const next = new Set(prev)
51	      if (next.has(page)) next.delete(page); else next.add(page)
52	      return next
53	    })
54	  }
55	
56	  const movePage = (fromPos: number, toPos: number) => {
57	    setPageOrder(prev => {
58	      const next = [...prev]
59	      const [page] = next.splice(fromPos, 1)
60	      next.splice(toPos, 0, page)
61	      return next
62	    })
63	  }
64	
65	  const resetForFile = (count: number, path: string, tmpl: string) => {
66	    setPdfPath(path)
67	    setPageCount(count)
68	    setPageOrder(Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => i + 1))
69	    setSelectedPage(1)
70	    setRotations(new Map())
71	    setSkipped(new Set())
72	    outputFiles.reset(applyTemplate(tmpl).value)
73	    focus.clear()
74	  }
75	
76	  useEffect(() => {
77	    if (!initialPath) return
78	    PageCount(initialPath)
79	      .then(count => resetForFile(count, initialPath, template))
80	      .catch(e => notifications.show({ title: 'Failed to open file', message: String(e), color: 'red' }))
81	  }, [initialPath])
82	
83	  const handleOpen = async () => {
84	    const path = await OpenPDF()
85	    if (!path) return
86	    try {
87	      const count = await PageCount(path)
88	      resetForFile(count, path, template)
89	    } catch (e) {
90	      notifications.show({ title: 'Failed to open file', message: String(e), color: 'red' })
91	    }
92	  }
93	
94	  const handlePickOutputFolder = async () => {
95	    const folder = await PickFolder()
96	    if (folder) setOutputFolder(folder)
97	  }
98	
99	  const handleExport = async () => {
100	    if (!pdfPath || !outputFolder) return
101	    setExporting(true)
102	    try {
103	      const sortedStarts = [...outputFiles.all.keys()].sort((a, b) => a - b)
104	      const files = sortedStarts.map((firstPos, i) => {
105	        const nextPos = sortedStarts[i + 1] ?? pageOrder.length
106	        const pages = pageOrder.slice(firstPos, nextPos).filter(p => !skipped.has(p))
107	        const f = outputFiles.all.get(firstPos)!
108	        return { pages, name: f.name, outDir: f.folderOverride ?? outputFolder! }
109	      })
110	      const conflicts = await CheckConflicts(files)
111	      if (conflicts.length > 0) {
112	        const names = conflicts.map(p => p.split('/').pop()).join(', ')
113	        notifications.show({ title: 'Filename conflict', message: `Already exists: ${names}`, color: 'orange', autoClose: false })
114	        return
115	      }
116	      await ExportSplit(pdfPath, files, Object.fromEntries(rotations))
117	      setSuccessModal({show: true, outputPath: outputFolder, inputPath: pdfPath})
118	    } catch (e) {
119	      notifications.show({ title: 'Export failed', message: String(e), color: 'red' })
120	    } finally {
121	      setExporting(false)
122	    }
123	  }
124	
125	  const closeSuccessModal = () => setSuccessModal({show: false, outputPath: '', inputPath: ''})
126	
127	  const handleDeleteInput = async () => {
128	    const { inputPath } = successModal
129	    closeSuccessModal()
130	    setPdfPath(null)
131	    try {
132	      await DeleteFile(inputPath)
133	    } catch (e) {
134	      notifications.show({ title: 'Failed to delete file', message: String(e), color: 'red' })
135	    }
136	  }
137	
138	  const handleToggleSplitPoint = useCallback((afterPosition: number) => {
139	    const prefill = applyTemplate(template)
140	    const added = outputFiles.toggle(afterPosition, prefill.value)
141	    if (added) focus.request(afterPosition, prefill.cursorPos)
142	    else focus.clear()
143	  }, [template, outputFiles.toggle])
144	
145	  return (
146	    <Box style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100%' }}>
147	      <Modal opened={successModal.show} onClose={closeSuccessModal} title="Export complete" centered>
148	        <Stack gap="md">
149	          <Group>
150	            <Text size="sm" c="dimmed" style={{ flex: 1 }}>{successModal.outputPath}</Text>
151	            <Button size="xs" variant="default" onClick={() => OpenFile(successModal.outputPath)}>
152	              Open in Finder
153	            </Button>
154	          </Group>
155	          <Divider />
156	          <div>
157	            <Text size="sm" mb={4}>What do you want to do with the input file?</Text>
158	            <Text size="sm" c="dimmed" mb="sm">{successModal.inputPath.split('/').pop()}</Text>
159	            <Group>
160	              <Button variant="default" onClick={closeSuccessModal}>Keep</Button>
161	              <Button color="red" onClick={handleDeleteInput}>Delete</Button>
162	            </Group>
163	          </div>
164	        </Stack>
165	      </Modal>
166	      <Box
167	        style={{
168	          flexShrink: 0,
169	          borderBottom: '1px solid var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',
170	          display: 'flex',
171	          alignItems: 'center',
172	          paddingInline: 12,
173	          height: 44,
174	        }}
175	      >
176	        <Group gap={8} style={{ width: '100%' }}>
177	          <Button size="xs" variant="default" onClick={handleOpen}>
178	            Open PDF
179	          </Button>
180	          <TextInput
181	            size="xs"
182	            placeholder={DEFAULT_TEMPLATE}
183	            value={template}
184	            onChange={(e) => setTemplate(e.currentTarget.value)}
185	            leftSection={<span style={{ fontSize: 11, whiteSpace: 'nowrap', color: 'var(--mantine-color-dimmed)' }}>Template</span>}
186	            leftSectionWidth={60}
187	            style={{ flex: 1 }}
188	          />
189	          <Button size="xs" variant="default" onClick={handlePickOutputFolder}>
190	            {outputFolder ? ellipsisPath(outputFolder) : 'Output folder…'}
191	          </Button>
192	          <Button size="xs" disabled={!pdfPath || !outputFolder || outputFiles.duplicateFirstPages.size > 0} loading={exporting} onClick={handleExport}>
193	            Export
194	          </Button>
195	        </Group>
196	      </Box>
197	
198	      <Box style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'hidden', display: 'flex' }}>
199	        {pdfPath ? (
200	          <>
201	            <ThumbnailPanel
202	              pdfPath={pdfPath}
203	              pageOrder={pageOrder}
204	              selectedPage={selectedPage}
205	              onSelectPage={setSelectedPage}
206	              onToggleSplitPoint={handleToggleSplitPoint}
207	              outputFiles={outputFiles}
208	              outputFolder={outputFolder}
209	              focus={focus}
210	              rotations={rotations}
211	              onRotate={rotate}
212	              skipped={skipped}
213	              onToggleSkip={toggleSkip}
214	              onMovePage={movePage}
215	            />
216	            <DetailPanel
217	              pdfPath={pdfPath}
218	              pageNum={selectedPage}
219	              pageCount={pageCount}
220	              rotation={rotations.get(selectedPage) ?? 0}
221	              onRotate={() => rotate(selectedPage)}
222	
223	            />
224	          </>
225	        ) : (
226	          <Box style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
227	            <Button onClick={handleOpen}>Open PDF</Button>
228	          </Box>
229	        )}
230	      </Box>
231	    </Box>
232	  )
233	}
