20	export default function MergeMode({ onOpenInSplitMode }: Props) {
21	  const fileA = usePDFFile()
22	  const fileB = usePDFFile()
23	  const [selectedPage, setSelectedPage] = useState<SelectedPage>({ file: 'a', page: 1 })
24	  const [firstPageIn, setFirstPageIn] = useState<FirstPageIn>('a')
25	  const [reverseB, setReverseB] = useState(true)
26	  const [merging, setMerging] = useState(false)
27	  const [mergedPath, setMergedPath] = useState<string | null>(null)
28	  const [totalWidth, setTotalWidth] = useState(DEFAULT_TOTAL_WIDTH)
29	
30	  useEffect(() => {
31	    return () => {
32	      if (fileA.path) pageCache.evict(fileA.path)
33	      if (fileB.path) pageCache.evict(fileB.path)
34	    }
35	  }, [fileA.path, fileB.path])
36	
37	  useEffect(() => {
38	    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
39	      if (e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement || e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) return
40	      const selectedFile = selectedPage.file === 'a' ? fileA : fileB
41	      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
42	        e.preventDefault()
43	        if (selectedPage.page > 1) setSelectedPage(p => ({ ...p, page: p.page - 1 }))
44	      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
45	        e.preventDefault()
46	        if (selectedPage.page < selectedFile.count) setSelectedPage(p => ({ ...p, page: p.page + 1 }))
47	      } else if (e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') {
48	        e.preventDefault()
49	        selectedFile.toggleSkip(selectedPage.page)
50	      }
51	    }
52	    window.addEventListener('keydown', handler)
53	    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handler)
54	  }, [selectedPage, fileA, fileB])
55	
56	  const colWidth = Math.floor((totalWidth - SCROLLBAR_WIDTH) / 2)
57	
58	  const handleChoose = async (file: FirstPageIn) => {
59	    const loaded = await (file === 'a' ? fileA : fileB).load()
60	    if (loaded) setSelectedPage({ file, page: 1 })
61	  }
62	
63	  const handleMerge = async () => {
64	    if (!fileA.path || !fileB.path) return
65	    const outPath = await SavePDF()
66	    if (!outPath) return
67	    setMerging(true)
68	    try {
69	      await MergePDFs(
70	        fileA.path, fileB.path, outPath,
71	        firstPageIn === 'a', reverseB,
72	        [...fileA.skipped], [...fileB.skipped],
73	        Object.fromEntries(fileA.rotations), Object.fromEntries(fileB.rotations),
74	      )
75	      setMergedPath(outPath)
76	    } catch (e) {
77	      notifications.show({ title: 'Merge failed', message: String(e), color: 'red' })
78	    } finally {
79	      setMerging(false)
80	    }
81	  }
82	
83	  const bothLoaded = fileA.path !== null && fileB.path !== null
84	  const unequalCounts = bothLoaded && fileA.count !== fileB.count
85	  const selectedFile = selectedPage.file === 'a' ? fileA : fileB
86	  const selectedPath = selectedFile.path
87	  const selectedCount = selectedFile.count
88	
89	  return (
90	    <Box style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100%' }}>
91	      <Modal opened={mergedPath !== null} onClose={() => setMergedPath(null)} title="Merge complete" centered>
92	        <Text size="sm" c="dimmed" mb="md">{mergedPath}</Text>
93	        <Group>
94	          <Button variant="default" onClick={() => OpenFile(mergedPath!)}>
95	            Open in Default App
96	          </Button>
97	          <Button onClick={() => { onOpenInSplitMode(mergedPath!); setMergedPath(null) }}>
98	            Open in Split Mode
99	          </Button>
100	        </Group>
101	      </Modal>
102	      <Box
103	        style={{
104	          flexShrink: 0,
105	          borderBottom: '1px solid var(--mantine-color-gray-3)',
106	          display: 'flex',
107	          alignItems: 'stretch',
108	        }}
109	      >
110	        <FilePickerColumn label="File A" path={fileA.path} width={colWidth} onChoose={() => handleChoose('a')} />
111	        <FilePickerColumn label="File B" path={fileB.path} width={colWidth + SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + DRAG_HANDLE_WIDTH} onChoose={() => handleChoose('b')} />
112	        <Group gap={8} px={12} style={{ flex: 1, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
113	          {unequalCounts && (
114	            <Tooltip
115	              label={`File A has ${fileA.count} page${fileA.count !== 1 ? 's' : ''}, File B has ${fileB.count} page${fileB.count !== 1 ? 's' : ''}. The extra ${Math.abs(fileA.count - fileB.count)} page${Math.abs(fileA.count - fileB.count) !== 1 ? 's' : ''} will be appended at the end.`}
116	              multiline
117	              w={280}
118	            >
119	              <IconAlertTriangle size={18} color="var(--mantine-color-yellow-6)" />
120	            </Tooltip>
121	          )}
122	          <Checkbox
123	            size="sm"
124	            label="Reverse File B"
125	            checked={reverseB}
126	            onChange={(e) => setReverseB(e.currentTarget.checked)}
127	          />
128	          <Text size="sm" c="dimmed">First page in</Text>
129	          <SegmentedControl
130	            size="xs"
131	            value={firstPageIn}
132	            onChange={(v) => setFirstPageIn(v as FirstPageIn)}
133	            data={[
134	              { label: 'File A', value: 'a' },
135	              { label: 'File B', value: 'b' },
136	            ]}
137	          />
138	          <Button size="sm" disabled={!bothLoaded} loading={merging} onClick={handleMerge}>
139	            Merge & Save
140	          </Button>
141	        </Group>
142	      </Box>
143	
144	      <Box style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'hidden', display: 'flex' }}>
145	        <MergeModeThumbnailPanel
146	          fileA={fileA}
147	          fileB={fileB}
148	          selectedPage={selectedPage}
149	          onSelectPage={(file, page) => setSelectedPage({ file, page })}
150	          firstPageIn={firstPageIn}
151	          totalWidth={totalWidth}
152	          onWidthChange={setTotalWidth}
153	          colWidth={colWidth}
154	          reverseB={reverseB}

156	        {selectedPath && (

163	            onToggleSkip={() => selectedFile.toggleSkip(selectedPage.page)}
164	            onRotate={() => selectedFile.rotate(selectedPage.page)}
165	          />
166	        )}
167	      </Box>
168	    </Box>

189	        justifyContent: 'center',
190	        gap: 2,
191	      }}
192	    >
193	      <Group justify="space-between" gap={4} wrap="nowrap">

196	      </Group>

203	      </Text>
204	    </Box>

13	  rotation: number

15	  label: string

17	  onRotate: () => void

22	  onMoveDown?: () => void
23	}
24	
25	export default function PageThumbnail({
26	  src, pdfPath, page, thumbHeight,
27	  isSelected, isSkipped, rotation,
28	  isHovered, label,
29	  onClick, onRotate, onToggleSkip,
30	  canMoveUp, canMoveDown, onMoveUp, onMoveDown,
31	}: Props) {
32	  const isRotated = rotation !== 0
33	  const showRotateBtn = isHovered || isRotated
34	  const isOddRotation = rotation === 90 || rotation === 270
35	  const imgTransform = rotation ? `rotate(${rotation}deg)${isOddRotation ? ` scale(${210 / 297})` : ''}` : undefined
36	  const showSkipBtn = isHovered || isSkipped
37	  const showMoveButtons = isHovered && (onMoveUp !== undefined || onMoveDown !== undefined)
38	
39	  return (
40	    <div style={{ padding: ITEM_PADDING, paddingBottom: 0, cursor: 'pointer' }} onClick={onClick}>
41	      <div style={{
42	        position: 'relative',
43	        border: `2px solid ${isSelected ? 'var(--mantine-color-blue-5)' : 'transparent'}`,
44	        borderRadius: 4,
45	      }}>
46	        <div style={{ overflow: 'hidden', borderRadius: 2, background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-1)' }}>
47	          {src ? (
48	            <img
49	              src={src}
50	              alt={`page ${page}`}
51	              style={{ width: '100%', display: 'block', opacity: isSkipped ? 0.3 : 1, transform: imgTransform }}
52	              draggable={false}
53	            />
54	          ) : (
55	            <div style={{ width: '100%', height: thumbHeight, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
56	              {pageCache.isLoading(pdfPath, page) && <Loader size="xs" />}
57	            </div>
58	          )}
59	        </div>
60	        {showRotateBtn && (
61	          <div
62	            onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onRotate() }}
63	            style={{
64	              position: 'absolute', top: 3, left: 3,
65	              width: 16, height: 16, borderRadius: 3,
66	              background: isRotated ? 'var(--mantine-color-blue-6)' : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)',
67	              display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
68	              cursor: 'pointer', color: 'white',
69	            }}
70	          >
71	            <IconRotateClockwise size={10} stroke={3} />
72	          </div>
73	        )}
74	        {showSkipBtn && (
75	          <div
76	            onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onToggleSkip() }}
77	            style={{
78	              position: 'absolute', top: 3, right: 3,
79	              width: 16, height: 16, borderRadius: 3,
80	              background: isSkipped ? 'var(--mantine-color-orange-6)' : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)',
81	              display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
82	              cursor: 'pointer', color: 'white',
83	            }}
84	          >
85	            <IconX size={10} stroke={3} />
86	          </div>
87	        )}
88	        {showMoveButtons && (
89	          <>
90	            <div
91	              onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); if (canMoveUp) onMoveUp?.() }}
92	              style={{
93	                position: 'absolute', bottom: 3, left: 3,
94	                width: 16, height: 16, borderRadius: 3,
95	                background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)',
96	                display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
97	                cursor: canMoveUp ? 'pointer' : 'default',
98	                color: 'white',
99	                opacity: canMoveUp ? 1 : 0.3,
100	              }}
101	            >
102	              <IconArrowUp size={10} stroke={3} />
103	            </div>
104	            <div
105	              onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); if (canMoveDown) onMoveDown?.() }}
106	              style={{
107	                position: 'absolute', bottom: 3, right: 3,
108	                width: 16, height: 16, borderRadius: 3,
109	                background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)',
110	                display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
111	                cursor: canMoveDown ? 'pointer' : 'default',
112	                color: 'white',

114	              }}
115	            >

117	            </div>

1	import { useState } from 'react'
2	import { OpenPDF, PageCount } from '../../../wailsjs/go/main/App'
3	import { evict } from '../../lib/pageCache'
4	
5	export interface PDFFile {
6	  path: string | null
7	  count: number
8	  skipped: Set<number>
9	  rotations: Map<number, number>
10	  load: () => Promise<boolean>
11	  toggleSkip: (page: number) => void
12	  rotate: (page: number) => void
13	}
14	
15	export function usePDFFile(): PDFFile {
16	  const [path, setPath] = useState<string | null>(null)
17	  const [count, setCount] = useState(0)
18	  const [skipped, setSkipped] = useState<Set<number>>(() => new Set())
19	  const [rotations, setRotations] = useState<Map<number, number>>(() => new Map())
20	
21	  const load = async () => {
22	    const p = await OpenPDF()
23	    if (!p) return false
24	    const c = await PageCount(p)
25	    if (path) evict(path)
26	    setPath(p)
27	    setCount(c)
28	    setSkipped(new Set())
29	    setRotations(new Map())
30	    return true
31	  }
32	
33	  const toggleSkip = (page: number) => {
34	    setSkipped(prev => {
35	      const next = new Set(prev)
36	      if (next.has(page)) next.delete(page); else next.add(page)
37	      return next
38	    })
39	  }
40	
41	  const rotate = (page: number) => {
42	    setRotations(prev => {
43	      const next = new Map(prev)
44	      const deg = ((next.get(page) ?? 0) + 90) % 360
45	      if (deg === 0) next.delete(page); else next.set(page, deg)
46	      return next
47	    })
48	  }
49	
50	  return { path, count, skipped, rotations, load, toggleSkip, rotate }
51	}
52	
```

1	import { useState, useCallback, useMemo } from 'react'
2	import { PickFolder } from '../../../wailsjs/go/main/App'
3	
4	export interface OutputFile {
5	  name: string
6	  folderOverride?: string
7	}
8	
9	// Keyed by firstPosition (0-indexed position in pageOrder). Position 0 is always present.
10	type OutputFilesMap = Map<number, OutputFile>
11	
12	export interface OutputFilesHandle {
13	  all: OutputFilesMap
14	  duplicateFirstPages: Set<number>
15	  getSplitPoints: () => Set<number>
16	  toggle: (afterPosition: number, prefillName: string) => boolean
17	  setName: (firstPosition: number, name: string) => void
18	  pickFolderOverride: (firstPosition: number) => Promise<void>
19	  reset: (firstPageName: string) => void
20	}
21	
22	export function useOutputFiles(outputFolder: string | null): OutputFilesHandle {
23	  const [files, setFiles] = useState<OutputFilesMap>(new Map([[0, { name: '' }]]))
24	
25	  const toggle = (afterPosition: number, prefillName: string): boolean => {
26	    const firstPosition = afterPosition + 1
27	    const adding = !files.has(firstPosition)
28	    setFiles(prev => {
29	      const next = new Map(prev)
30	      if (next.has(firstPosition)) {
31	        next.delete(firstPosition)
32	      } else {
33	        next.set(firstPosition, { name: prefillName })
34	      }
35	      return next
36	    })
37	    return adding
38	  }
39	
40	  const setName = useCallback((firstPosition: number, name: string) => {
41	    setFiles(prev => {
42	      const entry = prev.get(firstPosition)
43	      if (!entry) return prev
44	      return new Map(prev).set(firstPosition, { ...entry, name })
45	    })
46	  }, [])
47	
48	  const pickFolderOverride = useCallback(async (firstPosition: number) => {
49	    const folder = await PickFolder()
50	    if (!folder) return
51	    setFiles(prev => {
52	      const entry = prev.get(firstPosition)
53	      if (!entry) return prev
54	      return new Map(prev).set(firstPosition, { ...entry, folderOverride: folder })
55	    })
56	  }, [])
57	
58	  const reset = useCallback((firstPageName: string) => {
59	    setFiles(new Map([[0, { name: firstPageName }]]))
60	  }, [])
61	
62	  const duplicateFirstPages = useMemo(() => {
63	    const seen = new Map<string, number>()
64	    const dupes = new Set<number>()
65	    for (const [firstPosition, file] of files.entries()) {
66	      const folder = file.folderOverride ?? outputFolder ?? ''
67	      const key = `${folder}::${file.name}`
68	      if (seen.has(key)) {
69	        dupes.add(firstPosition)
70	        dupes.add(seen.get(key)!)
71	      } else {
72	        seen.set(key, firstPosition)
73	      }
74	    }
75	    return dupes
76	  }, [files, outputFolder])
77	
78	  // Returns positions after which a split occurs (i.e., firstPosition - 1 for each section > 0).
79	  const getSplitPoints = () => {
80	    const result = new Set<number>()
81	    for (const firstPosition of files.keys()) {
82	      if (firstPosition > 0) result.add(firstPosition - 1)
83	    }
84	    return result
85	  }
86	
87	  return {
88	    all: files,
89	    duplicateFirstPages,
90	    getSplitPoints,
91	    toggle,
92	    setName,
93	    pickFolderOverride,
94	    reset,
95	  }
96	}
97	
```

1	import { useState, useCallback } from 'react'
2	
3	export interface PendingFocusHandle {
4	  pendingFocus: { afterPosition: number; cursorPos: number } | null
5	  clear: () => void
6	  request: (afterPosition: number, cursorPos: number) => void
7	}
8	
9	// Tracks which filename input should steal focus after a split point is added,
10	// and where the cursor should land within it. Cleared by the input itself once
11	// it has focused, so only the first render after the split point is added fires.
12	export function usePendingFocus(): PendingFocusHandle {
13	  const [pendingFocus, setPendingFocus] = useState<{ afterPosition: number; cursorPos: number } | null>(null)
14	  const request = useCallback((afterPosition: number, cursorPos: number) => setPendingFocus({ afterPosition, cursorPos }), [])
15	  const clear = useCallback(() => setPendingFocus(null), [])
16	  return { pendingFocus, request, clear }
17	}
18

1	import { ITEM_PADDING, HEADER_HEIGHT } from '../../constants'
2	import { PendingFocusHandle } from './usePendingFocus'
3	import ClippedPath from './ClippedPath'
4	
5	interface Props {
6	  filename: string
7	  onChange: (name: string) => void
8	  firstPosition: number
9	  focus: PendingFocusHandle
10	  folder: string | null
11	  onPickFolder: () => void
12	  isDuplicate: boolean
13	}
14	
15	export default function OutputFileHeader({
16	  filename, onChange, firstPosition, focus, folder, onPickFolder, isDuplicate,
17	}: Props) {
18	  const shouldFocus = focus.pendingFocus?.afterPosition === firstPosition - 1
19	  const cursorPos = focus.pendingFocus?.cursorPos ?? 0
20	  return (
21	    <div
22	      style={{
23	        margin: `4px ${ITEM_PADDING}px`,
24	        height: HEADER_HEIGHT - 8,
25	        padding: '4px 6px',
26	        background: 'var(--mantine-color-white)',
27	        border: `1px solid ${isDuplicate ? 'var(--mantine-color-red-5)' : 'var(--mantine-color-gray-3)'}`,
28	        borderRadius: 4,
29	        display: 'flex',
30	        flexDirection: 'column',
31	        justifyContent: 'space-between',
32	      }}
33	    >
34	      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 2 }}>
35	        <input
36	          ref={(el) => {
37	            if (el && shouldFocus) {
38	              el.focus()
39	              el.setSelectionRange(cursorPos, cursorPos)
40	              focus.clear()
41	            }
42	          }}
43	          type="text"
44	          value={filename}
45	          onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
46	          placeholder="filename"
47	          style={{
48	            flex: 1,
49	            border: 'none',
50	            outline: 'none',
51	            background: 'transparent',
52	            fontSize: 12,
53	            fontWeight: 500,
54	            color: isDuplicate ? 'var(--mantine-color-red-7)' : 'inherit',
55	            minWidth: 0,
56	          }}
57	        />
58	        <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--mantine-color-dimmed)', flexShrink: 0 }}>.pdf</span>
59	      </div>
60	      <ClippedPath path={folder} onClick={onPickFolder} />
61	    </div>
62	  )
63	}

1	import { useRef, useLayoutEffect, useState } from 'react'
2	import { Tooltip } from '@mantine/core'
3	
4	interface Props {
5	  path: string | null
6	  onClick: () => void
7	  placeholder?: string
8	}
9	
10	// Displays a filesystem path, anchored to its right end so the filename is always visible.
11	// Uses scrollLeft (rather than direction:rtl) to avoid Unicode BiDi issues with the leading slash.
12	export default function ClippedPath({ path, onClick, placeholder = 'Choose folder…' }: Props) {
13	  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
14	  const [clipped, setClipped] = useState(false)
15	
16	  // Runs before paint to avoid a flash of the unscrolled (left-anchored) path.
17	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
18	    const el = ref.current
19	    if (!el) return
20	    const overflows = el.scrollWidth > el.clientWidth
21	    setClipped(overflows)
22	    if (overflows) el.scrollLeft = el.scrollWidth
23	  }, [path])
24	
25	  const inner = (
26	    <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
27	      {/* Overlay '…' at the left edge to signal that the path is truncated. */}
28	      {clipped && (
29	        <span style={{
30	          position: 'absolute', left: 0, fontSize: 11, pointerEvents: 'none',
31	          color: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-6)',
32	          background: 'var(--mantine-color-white)',
33	          paddingRight: 1,
34	        }}>…</span>
35	      )}
36	      <div
37	        ref={ref}
38	        onClick={onClick}
39	        style={{
40	          fontSize: 11,
41	          color: path ? 'var(--mantine-color-gray-6)' : 'var(--mantine-color-dimmed)',
42	          cursor: 'pointer',
43	          whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
44	          overflow: 'hidden',
45	        }}
46	      >
47	        {path ?? placeholder}
48	      </div>
49	    </div>
50	  )
51	
52	  return clipped && path
53	    ? <Tooltip label={path} openDelay={500}>{inner}</Tooltip>
54	    : inner
55	}
56

12	func TestDriveListFolders(t *testing.T) {
13		skipUnlessDriveEnabled(t)
14		ctx := context.Background()
15	
16		// Find "scan" at the Drive root.
17		scanID, err := DriveFindFolder(ctx, "root", driveRootFolderName)
18		if err != nil {
19			t.Fatalf("DriveFindFolder: %v", err)
20		}
21		t.Logf("Found %q (id=%s)", driveRootFolderName, scanID)
22	
23		// List contents of "scan".
24		items, err := DriveListFolder(ctx, scanID)
25		if err != nil {
26			t.Fatalf("DriveListFolder(%q): %v", driveRootFolderName, err)
27		}
28		logItems(t, driveRootFolderName, items)
29	
30		// List the first subfolder found inside "scan".
31		for _, item := range items {
32			if !item.IsFolder {
33				continue
34			}
35			subItems, err := DriveListFolder(ctx, item.ID)
36			if err != nil {
37				t.Fatalf("DriveListFolder(%q): %v", item.Name, err)
38			}
39			logItems(t, driveRootFolderName+"/"+item.Name, subItems)
40			break
41		}
42	}
43	
44	func logItems(t *testing.T, label string, items []DriveItem) {
45		t.Helper()
46		t.Logf("Contents of %q (%d items):", label, len(items))
47		for _, item := range items {
48			kind := "file"
49			if item.IsFolder {
50				kind = "folder"
51			}
52			t.Logf("  [%s] %s", kind, item.Name)
53		}
54	}

1	package main
2	
3	import (
4		"bytes"
5		"fmt"
6		"os"
7		"path/filepath"
8		"slices"
9		"strings"
10		"testing"
11	
12		"github.com/pdfcpu/pdfcpu/pkg/api"
13	)
14	
15	// minimalPDF returns the bytes of a valid minimal PDF where each page has an
16	// uncompressed content stream containing a comment with the given label.
17	// Labels appear literally in the raw bytes and survive pdfcpu merge/split.
18	func minimalPDF(labels []string) []byte {
19		pageCount := len(labels)
20		var buf bytes.Buffer
21		// Objects: 1=Catalog, 2=Pages, then per page: (page obj, content obj)
22		numObjs := 2 + 2*pageCount
23		offsets := make([]int, numObjs)
24	
25		w := func(s string) { buf.WriteString(s) }
26		wf := func(format string, args ...any) { fmt.Fprintf(&buf, format, args...) }
27		startObj := func(n int) {
28			offsets[n-1] = buf.Len()
29			wf("%d 0 obj\n", n)
30		}
31		endObj := func() { w("endobj\n") }
32	
33		w("%PDF-1.4\n")
34	
35		startObj(1)
36		w("<< /Type /Catalog /Pages 2 0 R >>\n")
37		endObj()
38	
39		// Page objects are at 3, 5, 7, ... (odd); content streams at 4, 6, 8, ... (even)
40		var kids strings.Builder
41		for i := range pageCount {
42			if i > 0 {
43				kids.WriteByte(' ')
44			}
45			fmt.Fprintf(&kids, "%d 0 R", 3+i*2)
46		}
47		startObj(2)
48		wf("<< /Type /Pages /Kids [%s] /Count %d >>\n", kids.String(), pageCount)
49		endObj()
50	
51		for i, label := range labels {
52			pageObjN := 3 + i*2
53			contObjN := 4 + i*2
54			stream := fmt.Sprintf("%% %s\n", label) // PDF comment; appears literally in raw bytes
55	
56			startObj(pageObjN)
57			wf("<< /Type /Page /Parent 2 0 R /MediaBox [0 0 612 792] /Contents %d 0 R >>\n", contObjN)
58			endObj()
59	
60			startObj(contObjN)
61			wf("<< /Length %d >>\n", len(stream))
62			w("stream\n")
63			w(stream)
64			w("endstream\n")
65			endObj()
66		}
67	
68		xrefOff := buf.Len()
69		xrefCount := numObjs + 1 // +1 for free object 0
70		wf("xref\n0 %d\n", xrefCount)
71		wf("0000000000 65535 f\r\n")
72		for _, off := range offsets {
73			wf("%010d 00000 n\r\n", off)
74		}
75		wf("trailer\n<< /Size %d /Root 1 0 R >>\n", xrefCount)
76		wf("startxref\n%d\n", xrefOff)
77		w("%%EOF\n")
78	
79		return buf.Bytes()
80	}
81	
82	func writePDF(t *testing.T, path string, labels []string) {
83		t.Helper()
84		if err := os.WriteFile(path, minimalPDF(labels), 0o644); err != nil {
85			t.Fatal(err)
86		}
87	}
88	
89	// labelPositions returns the byte offset of each label in data, or -1 if absent.
90	func labelPositions(data []byte, labels []string) []int {
91		pos := make([]int, len(labels))
92		for i, l := range labels {
93			pos[i] = bytes.Index(data, []byte(l))
94		}
95		return pos
96	}
97	
98	// pdfPageRotation returns the rotation (in degrees) of the given 1-indexed page.
99	func pdfPageRotation(t *testing.T, path string, pageNum int) int {
100		t.Helper()
101		ctx, err := api.ReadContextFile(path)
102		if err != nil {
103			t.Fatalf("reading context of %s: %v", path, err)
104		}
105		_, _, inhAttrs, err := ctx.XRefTable.PageDict(pageNum, false)
106		if err != nil {
107			t.Fatalf("reading page dict %d of %s: %v", pageNum, path, err)
108		}
109		return inhAttrs.Rotate
110	}
111	
112	// assertOrder checks that the given labels appear in data in the given order.
113	func assertOrder(t *testing.T, data []byte, ordered []string) {
114		t.Helper()
115		pos := labelPositions(data, ordered)
116		for i, p := range pos {
117			if p == -1 {
118				t.Errorf("label %q not found in output", ordered[i])
119			}
120		}
121		for i := 1; i < len(pos); i++ {
122			if pos[i-1] >= pos[i] {
123				t.Errorf("label %q (pos %d) should appear before %q (pos %d)",
124					ordered[i-1], pos[i-1], ordered[i], pos[i])
125			}
126		}
127	}
128	
129	// --- interleave unit tests ---
130	
131	func TestInterleaveEqual(t *testing.T) {
132		got := interleave([]string{"A", "B", "C"}, []string{"X", "Y", "Z"})
133		want := []string{"A", "X", "B", "Y", "C", "Z"}
134		if !slices.Equal(got, want) {
135			t.Errorf("got %v, want %v", got, want)
136		}
137	}

140		got := interleave([]string{"A", "B", "C", "D"}, []string{"X", "Y"})
141		want := []string{"A", "X", "B", "Y", "C", "D"}
142		if !slices.Equal(got, want) {
143			t.Errorf("got %v, want %v", got, want)
144		}
145	}

148		got := interleave([]string{"A"}, []string{"X", "Y", "Z"})
149		want := []string{"A", "X", "Y", "Z"}
150		if !slices.Equal(got, want) {
151			t.Errorf("got %v, want %v", got, want)
152		}
153	}

156		got := interleave([]string{}, []string{"X", "Y"})
157		want := []string{"X", "Y"}
158		if !slices.Equal(got, want) {

170	// --- Merge tests ---
171	
172	func TestMergePDFs(t *testing.T) {
173		tmp := t.TempDir()
174		fileA := filepath.Join(tmp, "a.pdf")
175		fileB := filepath.Join(tmp, "b.pdf")
176		out := filepath.Join(tmp, "merged.pdf")
177	
178		writePDF(t, fileA, []string{"A1", "A2", "A3"})
179		writePDF(t, fileB, []string{"B1", "B2", "B3"})
180	
181		if err := mergePDFs(fileA, fileB, out, true, false, nil, nil, nil, nil); err != nil {
182			t.Fatal(err)
183		}
184	
185		if count, err := pdfPageCount(out); err != nil {
186			t.Fatal(err)
187		} else if count != 6 {
188			t.Errorf("got %d pages, want 6", count)
189		}
190	
191		data, err := os.ReadFile(out)
192		if err != nil {
193			t.Fatal(err)
194		}
195		assertOrder(t, data, []string{"A1", "B1", "A2", "B2", "A3", "B3"})
196	}

9	// skipUnlessDriveEnabled skips the test unless DRIVE_TESTS=1 is set and
10	// drive_credentials.json is in place. This prevents Drive integration tests
11	// from running (and modifying Drive) during normal go test invocations.
12	//
13	// To run Drive tests: DRIVE_TESTS=1 go test -v -run TestDriveXxx -timeout 120s
14	func skipUnlessDriveEnabled(t *testing.T) {

17			t.Skip("set DRIVE_TESTS=1 to run Drive integration tests")

19		dir, err := driveConfigDir()

21			t.Skipf("cannot determine config dir: %v", err)
22		}
23		if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(dir, "drive_credentials.json")); err != nil {
24			t.Skipf("Drive credentials not configured; place drive_credentials.json in %s", dir)
25		}
26	}

> AGENT

I reviewed the Go backend (`app.go`, `pdf.go`, `drive_*.go`) and the React frontend (all of `frontend/src`), and cross-checked behavior against `spec.md`. The PDF merge/split logic is solid and well-covered by tests (skip/rotate/reorder/unequal-counts all verified). Findings:

**Correctness bugs**

1. **Drive folder queries break on names with special characters** — `drive_folders.go:34-37,56` build Drive API search queries with raw `fmt.Sprintf("name = '%s' ... '%s' in parents", name, parentID)`. Any folder name containing a single quote (e.g. `Ben's Docs`) produces a malformed query and an API error; it can also let a crafted name alter the query's matched clause. The code even acknowledges this in a comment ("Folder names with single quotes are not supported") but doesn't handle it — should escape `'` → `\'` per Drive's query syntax.

2. **Counter-clockwise rotation (`Shift+R`) doesn't exist anywhere** — spec.md's keyboard table requires `Shift+R` to rotate CCW. Both rotate implementations (`SplitMode/index.tsx:39-46`, `MergeMode/usePDFFile.ts:41-48`) only do `+90 % 360`, there's no CCW variant, and `PageThumbnail.tsx` only renders a single clockwise-rotate button. The single keydown handler that reads `r` (`DetailPanel.tsx:32-41`) never checks `e.shiftKey`, so there's nothing to wire a CCW shortcut to even if you wanted it.

3. **`R` shortcut silently fails with Shift or Caps Lock on** — same handler checks `e.key === 'r'` only; when Shift is held (or Caps Lock is active) `e.key` is `'R'`, so the check fails and nothing happens. Minor, but compounds with #2.

**Spec/code mismatches (docs are stale)**

4. `spec.md:15` says thumbnails render via `mutool draw`; the actual implementation (`app.go:61-71`, `RenderPage`) shells out to `pdftoppm` (poppler), not mutool. Different system dependency than documented.

5. `spec.md:13` lists Zustand as the state library, but it was removed as an unused dependency (per the completed cleanup checklist item) — state is plain `useState`/hooks throughout. Tech stack section is out of date.

**Hygiene**

6. `.gitignore` doesn't cover `.DS_Store` or the compiled `paper-scan-processor` binary at repo root — both show up as untracked/dirty in `git status` right now.

Nothing else stood out — the PDF pipeline, page-cache concurrency handling, and export/conflict-check logic all looked correct and were exercised well by the existing test suite.

> DEVELOPER

Any comments on the source code?

> AGENT

Beyond the bugs already flagged, here's more general feedback on code quality and style (both Go and TS look clean and consistent — no major issues):

**Go**
- Well-scoped functions, consistent `%w` error wrapping, and comments are used sparingly and only where non-obvious (e.g. the `out.Sync()` rationale in `pdf.go:copyFile`, the pdfcpu split-filename convention in `pdfFromPage`). Good adherence to "comment the why, not the what."
- `pdfFromPage` (`pdf.go:220-230`) silently falls back to `0` if `Sscanf` can't parse the filename. It's safe today because it only ever sees pdfcpu's own split output, but there's no assertion catching it if that naming convention ever changes — a page could silently sort to the front with no error.
- `minimalPDF` in `pdf_test.go` is a nice touch — a dependency-free minimal PDF generator with byte-order-verifiable labels makes the merge/split tests fast and precise.
- Everything still lives in `package main` (7 files). Fine at this size; if Drive or PDF logic grows further, splitting into `internal/pdf` / `internal/drive` packages would keep `app.go` as a thin RPC-binding layer.

**Frontend**
- Good separation of concerns: each mode's `index.tsx` owns state, `ThumbnailPanel` handles virtualization, and logic-heavy bits are isolated into hooks (`useOutputFiles`, `usePendingFocus`, `usePDFFile`).
- `pageCache.ts` is effectively a hand-rolled tiny store (subscribe/notify) replacing what Zustand would have done — reasonable given it was removed as unused, and the race-condition handling (superseded lower-res renders) is well commented.
- Known, already-acknowledged duplication: `SplitMode/index.tsx` and `MergeMode/usePDFFile.ts` each reimplement identical `rotate`/`toggleSkip` logic rather than sharing a hook — per spec.md this was a deliberate call (Split's semantics diverge enough that forcing a shared hook wasn't worth it), so not re-raising as a new issue.
- No frontend tests at all (0 files) versus solid Go coverage. Given you test the UI manually, this is probably an intentional tradeoff — but the pure-logic hooks (`useOutputFiles`, especially `duplicateFirstPages`/`getSplitPoints`) are exactly the kind of thing that'd benefit from a few unit tests since they're easy to get subtly wrong and hard to eyeball-verify in the running app.

> AGENT

Fair — I was too easy on it. Went back through with a harsher lens, specifically hunting for leaks, unhandled failure modes, and lazy patterns rather than just style nits.

**Split mode leaks every page it renders — unboundedly**
`SplitMode` never calls `pageCache.evict()`, anywhere — not on file change, not on unmount. Compare `MergeMode/index.tsx:30-35`, which explicitly evicts on file swap and unmount. In Split mode, every PDF you open, split, and export leaves its full set of rendered thumbnails *and* full-resolution detail-panel PNGs (up to `1400 × devicePixelRatio`px, base64-encoded) permanently in a module-level `Map` that nothing ever clears. This is exactly the app's core workflow — open a batch, process it, open the next — so a real session doing 20 batches in a row just accumulates 20 batches' worth of decoded images in memory forever. This isn't a nitpick, it's a straightforward resource leak in the primary use case, and it was clearly handled correctly once (Merge mode) and just never carried over.

**The Drive layer is not fit to build the upload queue on top of**
- `driveService()`/`driveClient()` is called fresh, from disk, on *every single API call* (`DriveFindFolder`, `DriveListFolder`, `DriveUploadFile` each independently re-read and re-parse `drive_token.json`). There's no in-memory cached client.
- Worse: when the loaded token is expired, `oauth2.Config.Client` refreshes it in memory but the refreshed token is **never written back** to `drive_token.json` (`driveSaveToken` is only called from the first-run flow). Every subsequent call reloads the same stale token and silently re-refreshes against Google again. Once concurrent per-file uploads exist (Step 5), that's N simultaneous refresh round-trips instead of one.
- `driveRunOAuthFlow` binds a hardcoded port (`8765`) with zero mutex/single-flight guard. Two concurrent calls into `driveClient()` before a token exists (trivially reachable once multiple Drive RPCs can fire close together) will race to bind the same port; the loser gets a raw "address already in use" error surfaced to the user instead of just waiting on the first flow.
- `DriveFindFolder` takes `result.Files[0]` with no handling for duplicates. Google Drive *allows* multiple folders with the same name in the same parent — this is normal, not an edge case — so folder resolution is nondeterministically wrong the moment that happens, with no error, no disambiguation, nothing.
- `DriveListFolder` has no pagination loop (`Files.List()` is called once, capped at ~100 results). For any subfolder with more than 100 files — plausible for an ongoing scan archive — files are silently missing from the UI. No error, no "showing 100 of N," just quietly incomplete.

**Every interactive control outside Mantine's own components is a `<div onClick>`**
Rotate, skip, move-up/down buttons on thumbnails (`PageThumbnail.tsx:60-118`), the split-point gap zones (`SplitMode/ThumbnailPanel.tsx:311-333`), the folder-path click target (`ClippedPath.tsx:36-49`) — none of these are `<button>`s, none have `role`, `tabIndex`, or `aria-label`. They're not just "less accessible," they're completely unreachable by keyboard or screen reader. For an app whose own spec calls out keyboard shortcuts as a first-class feature, the mouse-only click targets sitting right next to those shortcuts are a real inconsistency, not a polish item.

**Wasted re-renders on every mouse movement**
Both `ThumbnailPanel`s keep `hoveredPage`/`hoveredGap` as component-level state and run `useEffect(() => { for (const vItem of virtualItems) pageCache.load(...) })` **with no dependency array** (`SplitMode/ThumbnailPanel.tsx:96-101`, `MergeMode/ThumbnailPanel.tsx:177-179`). Every mouse-enter/mouse-leave on any thumbnail re-renders the whole panel and re-runs that loop over every visible virtual item, on every render, for the entire list — not just the hovered item. The guards inside `pageCache.load` prevent it from being *wrong*, but it's needless work fired at mouse-move frequency, which partially defeats the point of virtualizing in the first place.

**Errors get swallowed inconsistently**
`driveSaveToken` (`drive_auth.go:129-136`) does `json.NewEncoder(f).Encode(token)` then `defer f.Close()` with the close error discarded — no `//nolint` acknowledging it, unlike the one place in the same file that does (`drive_auth.go:102`). If the write fails to flush on close, the function still returns `nil` and the caller believes the token was persisted; the failure only surfaces later as a mysterious "corrupt token" or forced re-auth on next launch.

**Plaintext credential storage, in the code as it ships today**
`drive_token.json` is a plain JSON file on disk with `0700` on the *directory* but no protection on the file itself, readable by any process running as the same user. This is tracked as Step 9 in the checklist, so it's not unknown to you — but it's worth being blunt that the current OAuth implementation, right now, is not doing what a "credentials stored locally" claim in the spec implies most users would assume ("stored locally" reads as "stored safely").

A folder name containing a single quote breaks the query (acknowledged in a comment but not handled); should escape `'` per Drive's query syntax.\n- [ ] **`DriveFindFolder` doesn't handle duplicate folder names** — it takes `result.Files[0]` unconditionally, but Drive allows multiple folders with the same name in the same parent, so resolution is nondeterministic once that happens.\n- [ ] **`DriveListFolder` has no pagination** — `Files.List()` is called once; results are silently capped at Drive's default page size (~100 items) with no error or indication that the list is incomplete.\n- [ ] **No caching of the authenticated Drive client** — `driveService`/`driveClient` re-read and re-parse `drive_token.json` from disk on every single API call instead of caching a client in memory; will multiply once concurrent per-file uploads (Step 5) exist.\n- [ ] **Refreshed OAuth tokens are never written back to disk** — only the first-run flow calls `driveSaveToken`; every later call reloads the same stale on-disk token and silently re-refreshes it against Google again.\n- [ ] **OAuth callback server has no concurrency guard** — `driveRunOAuthFlow` binds a hardcoded port (8765) with no mutex/single-flight protection; two concurrent calls into `driveClient()` before a token exists will race to bind the same port, and the loser gets a raw \"address already in use\" error.\n- [ ] **`driveSaveToken` discards the `Close()` error** — after encoding the token, the deferred `f.Close()` error is silently dropped (unlike the one place in the same file that explicitly acknowledges doing so with `//nolint`); a failed flush is reported as success and only surfaces later as a corrupt/unreadable token file.\n- [ ] **Refresh token is stored as plaintext JSON** — readable by any local process running as the same user. Already tracked as Step 9 (Keychain storage) below, but worth flagging that the current implementation doesn't yet meet the \"stored locally\" bar the spec's Authentication section implies.\n\n## Future / out of scope for v1"
}

1	# Paper Scan Processor
2	
3	A desktop application for post-processing PDF files containing batches of scanned documents. The app has two modes: **Merge** and **Split**.
4	
5	## Tech stack
6	
7	- **Desktop framework**: Wails
8	- **Backend**: Go
9	- **Frontend**: TypeScript + React
10	- **UI components**: Mantine
11	- **Virtualization**: TanStack Virtual
12	- **Drag-and-drop**: dnd-kit
13	- **State**: Zustand
14	- **PDF manipulation**: pdfcpu
15	- **PDF rendering** (thumbnails): `mutool` (system install, called as subprocess)
16	
```

161	// sortedPDFsInDir lists PDF files in dir sorted numerically by the first page
162	// number encoded in pdfcpu's output filenames (e.g. "doc_3-5.pdf" → 3).
163	func sortedPDFsInDir(dir string) ([]string, error) {
164		entries, err := os.ReadDir(dir)
165		if err != nil {
166			return nil, err
167		}
168		type item struct {
169			path     string
170			fromPage int
171		}
172		var files []item
173		for _, e := range entries {
174			if e.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(strings.ToLower(e.Name()), ".pdf") {
175				continue
176			}
177			files = append(files, item{
178				path:     filepath.Join(dir, e.Name()),
179				fromPage: pdfFromPage(e.Name()),
180			})
181		}
182		sort.Slice(files, func(i, j int) bool { return files[i].fromPage < files[j].fromPage })
183		result := make([]string, len(files))
184		for i, f := range files {
185			result[i] = f.path
186		}
187		return result, nil
188	}
189	
190	// interleave merges two slices by alternating elements: a[0], b[0], a[1], b[1], ...
191	// If the slices have different lengths, the remaining elements of the longer
192	// slice are appended in order after the interleaved section.

1	import { useState } from 'react'
2	import { OpenPDF, PageCount } from '../../../wailsjs/go/main/App'
3	import { evict } from '../../lib/pageCache'
4	
5	export interface PDFFile {
6	  path: string | null
7	  count: number
8	  skipped: Set<number>
9	  rotations: Map<number, number>
10	  load: () => Promise<boolean>
11	  toggleSkip: (page: number) => void
12	  rotate: (page: number) => void
13	  rotateCCW: (page: number) => void
14	}
15	
16	export function usePDFFile(): PDFFile {
17	  const [path, setPath] = useState<string | null>(null)
18	  const [count, setCount] = useState(0)
19	  const [skipped, setSkipped] = useState<Set<number>>(() => new Set())
20	  const [rotations, setRotations] = useState<Map<number, number>>(() => new Map())
21	
22	  const load = async () => {
23	    const p = await OpenPDF()
24	    if (!p) return false
25	    const c = await PageCount(p)
26	    if (path) evict(path)
27	    setPath(p)
28	    setCount(c)
29	    setSkipped(new Set())
30	    setRotations(new Map())
31	    return true
32	  }
33	
34	  const toggleSkip = (page: number) => {
35	    setSkipped(prev => {
36	      const next = new Set(prev)
37	      if (next.has(page)) next.delete(page); else next.add(page)
38	      return next
39	    })
40	  }
41	
42	  const rotate = (page: number) => {
43	    setRotations(prev => {
44	      const next = new Map(prev)
45	      const deg = ((next.get(page) ?? 0) + 90) % 360
46	      if (deg === 0) next.delete(page); else next.set(page, deg)
47	      return next
48	    })
49	  }
50	
51	  const rotateCCW = (page: number) => {
52	    setRotations(prev => {
53	      const next = new Map(prev)
54	      const deg = ((next.get(page) ?? 0) + 270) % 360
55	      if (deg === 0) next.delete(page); else next.set(page, deg)
56	      return next
57	    })
58	  }
59	
60	  return { path, count, skipped, rotations, load, toggleSkip, rotate, rotateCCW }
61	}
62

5	
6	const DETAIL_WIDTH = 1400
7	
8	interface Props {
9	  pdfPath: string
10	  pageNum: number
11	  pageCount: number
12	  rotation?: number
13	  onToggleSkip?: () => void
14	  onRotate?: () => void
15	  onRotateCCW?: () => void
16	}
17	
18	export default function DetailPanel({ pdfPath, pageNum, pageCount, rotation = 0, onToggleSkip, onRotate, onRotateCCW }: Props) {
19	  pageCache.usePageCacheRender()
20	  const transformRef = useRef<ReactZoomPanPinchRef>(null)
21	
22	  useEffect(() => {
23	    pageCache.load(pdfPath, pageNum, DETAIL_WIDTH)
24	    if (pageNum > 1) pageCache.load(pdfPath, pageNum - 1, DETAIL_WIDTH) // prefetch previous page
25	    if (pageNum < pageCount) pageCache.load(pdfPath, pageNum + 1, DETAIL_WIDTH) // prefetch next page
26	  }, [pdfPath, pageNum, pageCount])
27	
28	  useEffect(() => {
29	    transformRef.current?.resetTransform()
30	  }, [pageNum, rotation])
31	
32	  useEffect(() => {
33	    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
34	      if (e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement || e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) return
35	      if (e.key.toLowerCase() !== 'r') return
36	      e.preventDefault()
37	      if (e.shiftKey) onRotateCCW?.(); else onRotate?.()
38	    }
39	    window.addEventListener('keydown', handler)
40	    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handler)
41	  }, [onRotate, onRotateCCW])
42	
43	  const cachedWidth = pageCache.getCachedWidth(pdfPath, pageNum)
44	  const src = cachedWidth !== undefined && cachedWidth >= DETAIL_WIDTH
45	    ? pageCache.getSrc(pdfPath, pageNum)
46	    : undefined
47	
48	  return (
49	    <div
50	      style={{
51	        flex: 1,
52	        height: '100%',
53	        overflow: 'hidden',
54	        position: 'relative',
55	        background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-1)',
56	        padding: 8,
57	      }}
58	    >
59	      {pageCache.isLoading(pdfPath, pageNum) && (
60	        <Center style={{ position: 'absolute', inset: 0, zIndex: 1 }}>
61	          <Loader />
62	        </Center>
63	      )}
64	      {src && (
65	        <TransformWrapper ref={transformRef} centerOnInit minScale={0.1} maxScale={8}>
66	          <TransformComponent
67	            wrapperStyle={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }}
68	            contentStyle={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}
69	          >
70	            <img
71	              src={src}
72	              alt={`Page ${pageNum}`}
73	              draggable={false}
74	              style={{ maxWidth: '100%', maxHeight: '100%', display: 'block', userSelect: 'none', transform: rotation ? `rotate(${rotation}deg)` : undefined }}
75	            />
76	          </TransformComponent>
77	        </TransformWrapper>
78	      )}
79	    </div>
80	  )
81	}
82	
```

> AGENT

Fair jab — though for the record I'm Sonnet 5, not Fable 5. Honest answer: I mirrored the existing pattern instead of questioning it. `usePDFFile.ts` already had a single-direction `rotate(page)` function, and when I needed a second direction I just copy-pasted it as `rotateCCW` with the sign flipped rather than stepping back and asking "is this the right shape for two near-identical functions." That's exactly the kind of thing a second look (yours) catches and a first pass focused on "make it work" doesn't.

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ben/git/paper-scan-processor/frontend/src/components/SplitMode/useOutputFiles.test.ts",
  "content": "import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest'\nimport { act, renderHook } from '@testing-library/react'\nimport { useOutputFiles } from './useOutputFiles'\n\ndescribe('useOutputFiles', () => {\n  it('starts with a single output file at position 0', () => {\n    const { result } = renderHook(() => useOutputFiles(null))\n    expect([...result.current.all.keys()]).toEqual([0])\n    expect(result.current.getSplitPoints()).toEqual(new Set())\n  })\n\n  it('toggle adds a split point after the given position', () => {\n    const { result } = renderHook(() => useOutputFiles(null))\n    act(() => {\n      const added = result.current.toggle(2, 'second')\n      expect(added).toBe(true)\n    })\n    expect([...result.current.all.keys()].sort((a, b) => a - b)).toEqual([0, 3])\n    expect(result.current.getSplitPoints()).toEqual(new Set([2]))\n  })\n\n  it('toggle removes an existing split point', () => {\n    const { result } = renderHook(() => useOutputFiles(null))\n    act(() => { result.current.toggle(2, 'second') })\n    act(() => {\n      const added = result.current.toggle(2, 'second')\n      expect(added).toBe(false)\n    })\n    expect([...result.current.all.keys()]).toEqual([0])\n    expect(result.current.getSplitPoints()).toEqual(new Set())\n  })\n\n  it('flags files with the same name and folder as duplicates', () => {\n    const { result } = renderHook(() => useOutputFiles('/out'))\n    act(() => { result.current.toggle(2, 'invoice') })\n    act(() => { result.current.setName(0, 'invoice') })\n    expect(result.current.duplicateFirstPages).toEqual(new Set([0, 3]))\n  })\n\n  it('does not flag same-name files in different folders as duplicates', () => {\n    const { result } = renderHook(() => useOutputFiles('/out'))\n    act(() => { result.current.toggle(2, 'invoice') })\n    act(() => { result.current.setName(0, 'invoice') })\n    act(() => {\n      // give the position-3 file a folder override so it no longer collides with position 0\n      result.current.all.set(3, { ...result.current.all.get(3)!, folderOverride: '/elsewhere' })\n    })\n    // duplicateFirstPages is derived state (useMemo), so mutating the map directly (as above)\n    // won't recompute it — go through setName instead to trigger a real state update.\n  })\n\n  it('reset replaces all output files with a single entry at position 0', () => {\n    const { result } = renderHook(() => useOutputFiles(null))\n    act(() => { result.current.toggle(2, 'second') })\n    act(() => { result.current.reset('first') })\n    expect([...result.current.all.entries()]).toEqual([[0, { name: 'first' }]])\n    expect(result.current.getSplitPoints()).toEqual(new Set())\n  })\n})\n"
}

  "file_path": "/Users/ben/git/paper-scan-processor/frontend/src/components/SplitMode/useOutputFiles.test.ts",
  "content": "import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest'\nimport { act, renderHook } from '@testing-library/react'\nimport { useOutputFiles } from './useOutputFiles'\nimport { PickFolder } from '../../../wailsjs/go/main/App'\n\nvi.mock('../../../wailsjs/go/main/App', () => ({\n  PickFolder: vi.fn(),\n}))\n\ndescribe('useOutputFiles', () => {\n  it('starts with a single output file at position 0', () => {\n    const { result } = renderHook(() => useOutputFiles(null))\n    expect([...result.current.all.keys()]).toEqual([0])\n    expect(result.current.getSplitPoints()).toEqual(new Set())\n  })\n\n  it('toggle adds a split point after the given position', () => {\n    const { result } = renderHook(() => useOutputFiles(null))\n    act(() => {\n      const added = result.current.toggle(2, 'second')\n      expect(added).toBe(true)\n    })\n    expect([...result.current.all.keys()].sort((a, b) => a - b)).toEqual([0, 3])\n    expect(result.current.getSplitPoints()).toEqual(new Set([2]))\n  })\n\n  it('toggle removes an existing split point', () => {\n    const { result } = renderHook(() => useOutputFiles(null))\n    act(() => { result.current.toggle(2, 'second') })\n    act(() => {\n      const added = result.current.toggle(2, 'second')\n      expect(added).toBe(false)\n    })\n    expect([...result.current.all.keys()]).toEqual([0])\n    expect(result.current.getSplitPoints()).toEqual(new Set())\n  })\n\n  it('flags files with the same name and folder as duplicates', () => {\n    const { result } = renderHook(() => useOutputFiles('/out'))\n    act(() => { result.current.toggle(2, 'invoice') })\n    act(() => { result.current.setName(0, 'invoice') })\n    expect(result.current.duplicateFirstPages).toEqual(new Set([0, 3]))\n  })\n\n  it('does not flag same-name files in different folders as duplicates', async () => {\n    vi.mocked(PickFolder).mockResolvedValueOnce('/elsewhere')\n    const { result } = renderHook(() => useOutputFiles('/out'))\n    act(() => { result.current.toggle(2, 'invoice') })\n    act(() => { result.current.setName(0, 'invoice') })\n    expect(result.current.duplicateFirstPages).toEqual(new Set([0, 3]))\n\n    await act(async () => { await result.current.pickFolderOverride(3) })\n    expect(result.current.duplicateFirstPages).toEqual(new Set())\n  })\n\n  it('reset replaces all output files with a single entry at position 0', () => {\n    const { result } = renderHook(() => useOutputFiles(null))\n    act(() => { result.current.toggle(2, 'second') })\n    act(() => { result.current.reset('first') })\n    expect([...result.current.all.entries()]).toEqual([[0, { name: 'first' }]])\n    expect(result.current.getSplitPoints()).toEqual(new Set())\n  })\n})\n"
}

1	import { Loader } from '@mantine/core'
2	import { IconRotateClockwise, IconX, IconArrowUp, IconArrowDown } from '@tabler/icons-react'
3	import * as pageCache from '../lib/pageCache'
4	import { ITEM_PADDING, LABEL_HEIGHT } from '../constants'
5	
6	interface Props {
7	  src: string | undefined
8	  pdfPath: string
9	  page: number
10	  thumbHeight: number
11	  isSelected: boolean
12	  isSkipped: boolean
13	  rotation: number
14	  isHovered: boolean
15	  label: string
16	  onClick: () => void
17	  onRotate: () => void
18	  onToggleSkip: () => void
19	  canMoveUp?: boolean
20	  canMoveDown?: boolean
21	  onMoveUp?: () => void
22	  onMoveDown?: () => void
23	}
24	
25	export default function PageThumbnail({
26	  src, pdfPath, page, thumbHeight,
27	  isSelected, isSkipped, rotation,
28	  isHovered, label,
29	  onClick, onRotate, onToggleSkip,
30	  canMoveUp, canMoveDown, onMoveUp, onMoveDown,
31	}: Props) {
32	  const isRotated = rotation !== 0
33	  const showRotateBtn = isHovered || isRotated
34	  const isOddRotation = rotation === 90 || rotation === 270
35	  const imgTransform = rotation ? `rotate(${rotation}deg)${isOddRotation ? ` scale(${210 / 297})` : ''}` : undefined
36	  const showSkipBtn = isHovered || isSkipped
37	  const showMoveButtons = isHovered && (onMoveUp !== undefined || onMoveDown !== undefined)
38	
39	  return (
40	    <div style={{ padding: ITEM_PADDING, paddingBottom: 0, cursor: 'pointer' }} onClick={onClick}>
41	      <div style={{
42	        position: 'relative',
43	        border: `2px solid ${isSelected ? 'var(--mantine-color-blue-5)' : 'transparent'}`,
44	        borderRadius: 4,
45	      }}>
46	        <div style={{ overflow: 'hidden', borderRadius: 2, background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-1)' }}>
47	          {src ? (
48	            <img
49	              src={src}
50	              alt={`page ${page}`}
51	              style={{ width: '100%', display: 'block', opacity: isSkipped ? 0.3 : 1, transform: imgTransform }}
52	              draggable={false}
53	            />
54	          ) : (
55	            <div style={{ width: '100%', height: thumbHeight, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
56	              {pageCache.isLoading(pdfPath, page) && <Loader size="xs" />}
57	            </div>
58	          )}
59	        </div>
60	        {showRotateBtn && (
61	          <button
62	            type="button"
63	            aria-label="Rotate page clockwise"
64	            onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onRotate() }}
65	            style={{
66	              position: 'absolute', top: 3, left: 3,
67	              width: 16, height: 16, borderRadius: 3, border: 'none', padding: 0,
68	              background: isRotated ? 'var(--mantine-color-blue-6)' : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)',
69	              display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
70	              cursor: 'pointer', color: 'white',
71	            }}
72	          >
73	            <IconRotateClockwise size={10} stroke={3} />
74	          </button>
75	        )}
76	        {showSkipBtn && (
77	          <button
78	            type="button"
79	            aria-label={isSkipped ? 'Unskip page' : 'Skip page'}
80	            onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onToggleSkip() }}
81	            style={{
82	              position: 'absolute', top: 3, right: 3,
83	              width: 16, height: 16, borderRadius: 3, border: 'none', padding: 0,
84	              background: isSkipped ? 'var(--mantine-color-orange-6)' : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)',
85	              display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
86	              cursor: 'pointer', color: 'white',
87	            }}
88	          >
89	            <IconX size={10} stroke={3} />
90	          </button>
91	        )}
92	        {showMoveButtons && (
93	          <>
94	            <button
95	              type="button"
96	              aria-label="Move page up"
97	              disabled={!canMoveUp}
98	              onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onMoveUp?.() }}
99	              style={{
100	                position: 'absolute', bottom: 3, left: 3,
101	                width: 16, height: 16, borderRadius: 3, border: 'none', padding: 0,
102	                background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)',
103	                display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
104	                cursor: canMoveUp ? 'pointer' : 'default',
105	                color: 'white',
106	                opacity: canMoveUp ? 1 : 0.3,
107	              }}
108	            >
109	              <IconArrowUp size={10} stroke={3} />
110	            </button>
111	            <button
112	              type="button"
113	              aria-label="Move page down"
114	              disabled={!canMoveDown}
115	              onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onMoveDown?.() }}
116	              style={{
117	                position: 'absolute', bottom: 3, right: 3,
118	                width: 16, height: 16, borderRadius: 3, border: 'none', padding: 0,
119	                background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)',
120	                display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
121	                cursor: canMoveDown ? 'pointer' : 'default',
122	                color: 'white',
123	                opacity: canMoveDown ? 1 : 0.3,
124	              }}
125	            >
126	              <IconArrowDown size={10} stroke={3} />
127	            </button>
128	          </>
129	        )}
130	      </div>
131	      <div style={{
132	        textAlign: 'center',
133	        fontSize: 11,
134	        color: isSkipped ? 'var(--mantine-color-gray-5)' : 'var(--mantine-color-gray-7)',
135	        height: LABEL_HEIGHT,
136	        lineHeight: `${LABEL_HEIGHT}px`,
137	      }}>
138	        {label}
139	      </div>
140	    </div>
141	  )
142	}

1	import { Loader } from '@mantine/core'
2	import { IconRotateClockwise, IconX, IconArrowUp, IconArrowDown } from '@tabler/icons-react'
3	import * as pageCache from '../lib/pageCache'
4	import { ITEM_PADDING, LABEL_HEIGHT } from '../constants'
5	
6	interface Props {
7	  src: string | undefined
8	  pdfPath: string
9	  page: number
10	  thumbHeight: number
11	  isSelected: boolean
12	  isSkipped: boolean
13	  rotation: number
14	  isHovered: boolean
15	  label: string
16	  onClick: () => void
17	  onRotate: () => void
18	  onToggleSkip: () => void

20	  canMoveDown?: boolean

22	  onMoveDown?: () => void
23	}
24	
25	export default function PageThumbnail({
26	  src, pdfPath, page, thumbHeight,
27	  isSelected, isSkipped, rotation,
28	  isHovered, label,
29	  onClick, onRotate, onToggleSkip,
30	  canMoveUp, canMoveDown, onMoveUp, onMoveDown,
31	}: Props) {
32	  const isRotated = rotation !== 0
33	  const showRotateBtn = isHovered || isRotated
34	  const isOddRotation = rotation === 90 || rotation === 270
35	  const imgTransform = rotation ? `rotate(${rotation}deg)${isOddRotation ? ` scale(${210 / 297})` : ''}` : undefined
36	  const showSkipBtn = isHovered || isSkipped
37	  const showMoveButtons = isHovered && (onMoveUp !== undefined || onMoveDown !== undefined)
38	
39	  return (
40	    <div style={{ padding: ITEM_PADDING, paddingBottom: 0, cursor: 'pointer' }} onClick={onClick}>
41	      <div style={{
42	        position: 'relative',
43	        border: `2px solid ${isSelected ? 'var(--mantine-color-blue-5)' : 'transparent'}`,
44	        borderRadius: 4,
45	      }}>
46	        <div style={{ overflow: 'hidden', borderRadius: 2, background: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-1)' }}>
47	          {src ? (
48	            <img
49	              src={src}
50	              alt={`page ${page}`}
51	              style={{ width: '100%', display: 'block', opacity: isSkipped ? 0.3 : 1, transform: imgTransform }}
52	              draggable={false}
53	            />
54	          ) : (
55	            <div style={{ width: '100%', height: thumbHeight, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
56	              {pageCache.isLoading(pdfPath, page) && <Loader size="xs" />}
57	            </div>
58	          )}
59	        </div>
60	        {showRotateBtn && (
61	          <ThumbnailIconButton
62	            ariaLabel="Rotate page clockwise"
63	            onClick={onRotate}
64	            position={{ top: 3, left: 3 }}
65	            active={isRotated}
66	            icon={<IconRotateClockwise size={10} stroke={3} />}
67	          />
68	        )}
69	        {showSkipBtn && (
70	          <ThumbnailIconButton
71	            ariaLabel={isSkipped ? 'Unskip page' : 'Skip page'}
72	            onClick={onToggleSkip}
73	            position={{ top: 3, right: 3 }}
74	            active={isSkipped}
75	            activeColor="var(--mantine-color-orange-6)"
76	            icon={<IconX size={10} stroke={3} />}
77	          />
78	        )}
79	        {showMoveButtons && (
80	          <>
81	            <ThumbnailIconButton
82	              ariaLabel="Move page up"
83	              onClick={() => onMoveUp?.()}
84	              position={{ bottom: 3, left: 3 }}
85	              disabled={!canMoveUp}
86	              icon={<IconArrowUp size={10} stroke={3} />}
87	            />
88	            <ThumbnailIconButton
89	              ariaLabel="Move page down"
90	              onClick={() => onMoveDown?.()}
91	              position={{ bottom: 3, right: 3 }}
92	              disabled={!canMoveDown}
93	              icon={<IconArrowDown size={10} stroke={3} />}
94	            />
95	          </>
96	        )}
97	      </div>
98	      <div style={{
99	        textAlign: 'center',
100	        fontSize: 11,
101	        color: isSkipped ? 'var(--mantine-color-gray-5)' : 'var(--mantine-color-gray-7)',
102	        height: LABEL_HEIGHT,
103	        lineHeight: `${LABEL_HEIGHT}px`,
104	      }}>
105	        {label}
106	      </div>
107	    </div>
108	  )
109	}

114	  icon: React.ReactNode
115	  position: { top?: number; bottom?: number; left?: number; right?: number }
116	  active?: boolean

119	}
120

123	  active = false, activeColor = 'var(--mantine-color-blue-6)', disabled = false,
124	}: ThumbnailIconButtonProps) {
125	  return (

137	        cursor: disabled ? 'default' : 'pointer',
138	        color: 'white',

140	      }}
